Conclusion: 现有聚合指标不足以衡量AI系统对实体的可见性偏差；PEBM框架可系统性识别与量化这些偏差；品牌幻觉悖论与幽灵制图机制为理解AI幻觉提供了新视角，对品牌管理和AI系统评估具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: AI-mediated answer systems increasingly determine how brands and organizations are represented to users. Existing approaches reduce visibility to mention rate or citation frequency. This paper argues that aggregate metrics are insufficient because entities exhibit systematically different AI visibility error profiles.
  We introduce Per-Entity Bias Mapping (PEBM): a ten-dimensional framework distinguishing raw from verified mentions. Three failure modes are identified: (1) underrepresented entities suffer invisibility due to weak knowledge graph presence; (2) large entities suffer the Brand Hallucination Paradox -- model familiarity creates stronger surfaces for plausible but incorrect completions; (3) CEE entities face a structural infrastructure gap across knowledge graphs, NER, and entity linking. A fourth dimension, Parametric-Retrieval Lag Asymmetry, describes divergence between retrieval-augmented and parametric memory update cycles.
  A full-scale empirical study (n=100 Hungarian B2B entities, 1,400 probe runs, 2,062 sources) finds Tier 1 brands produce 52.69% fabricated citations versus 37.87% for Tier 3 entities (+14.82 pp; p=1.67e-11), supporting the Brand Hallucination Paradox. Regulatory-framed queries elevate fabrication to 56.77% versus 37.59% baseline (+19.2 pp). We identify rejection-induced confabulation escalation: agentic quality filters function as hallucination accelerators in compliance contexts. We introduce ghost cartography as a unifying mechanism: entities in sparse latent regions produce confident output interpolated from neighboring dense regions, yielding a two-dimensional confabulation space (fabricated presence vs. frozen representation).

</details>


### [334] [LLM and Human Modes of Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21616)
*Shalom Lappin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型（LLMs）与人类在语言知识表征和现实世界推理规划两个信息域中的认知差异。LLMs在语言任务上表现优异但处理方式与人类不同，且在推理任务的学习和泛化方面效率低于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较LLMs与人类在信息处理和表征上的认知基础，探究LLMs能否达到或超越人类在认知任务上的表现，以及两者信息处理系统的异同点。

Method: 综述近期在两个信息域（语言知识表征、现实世界推理与规划）中的研究成果，对LLMs与人类进行对比分析。

Result: LLMs在语言应用上表现优异且流畅，但其处理语言内容的方式与人类有显著差异；在推理任务上，LLMs的学习和泛化效率整体低于人类。

Conclusion: LLMs与人类在认知加工上存在根本差异，尤其是在语言处理和推理泛化方面，LLMs尚无法完全模拟人类认知机制。

Abstract: Much work on the cognitive foundations of AI has focussed on comparisons between the ways in which Large Language Models (LLMs) and humans process information and represent it. One aspect of this comparison involves determining the extent to which LLMs can achieve or surpass human performance on a variety of cognitively interesting tasks. A second explores points of convergence and divergence between LLM and human systems for processing information. Here, I consider some recent research that has addressed both issues in two informational domains. The first is the representation of linguistic knowledge. The second is real world reasoning and planning. While LLMs frequently achieve impressive levels of performance and fluency on linguistic applications, they tend to handle linguistic content in ways that are distinct from human processing. They are also, for the most part, less efficient than humans in learning and generalisation for reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [335] [CulMind: Benchmarking Multimodal Understanding and Reasoning in Chinese Cultural Heritage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21618)
*Zhangwei Cao,Shuhan Fan,Yuting Wei,Jiajun Zhang,Yihang Peng,Qi Meng,Yangfu Zhu,Liangbin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了CulMind和CulMind-R基准，涵盖50个任务和24个推理子任务，用于评估多模态大语言模型在中国文化遗产中的细粒度推理能力，并提出了任务自适应指标ReaScore来评估推理过程的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中国文化遗产（CCH）基准主要关注最终答案的准确性，而对推理过程的准确性和完整性缺乏评估，存在评估维度单一的问题。

Method: 构建了CulMind（50个任务，覆盖100+博物馆藏品）和CulMind-R（24个推理子任务，自适应定义任务特定推理维度）；提出ReaScore指标，通过自动加权任务相关维度来评估推理质量。

Result: 在14个前沿多模态大语言模型上的实验显示，答案准确性与推理质量之间存在显著差距，尤其在困难任务上；任务自适应的维度选择与加权能更好地与专家判断对齐。

Conclusion: 该基准和指标支持更符合专家评估标准的中国文化遗产理解评测，并为更广泛的文化遗产评估提供了可迁移的参考框架。

Abstract: Evaluating Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in Chinese Cultural Heritage (CCH) requires fine-grained reasoning over visual, textual, stylistic, and historical clues. However, existing CCH benchmarks mainly emphasize final-answer accuracy, while the accuracy and completeness of reasoning processes remain underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce CulMind and CulMind-R: a high-quality benchmark for multimodal CCH covering 50 tasks from collections of more than 100 museums, and a 24-task reasoning subset that adaptively defines task-specific dimensions for reasoning process evaluation. To evaluate reasoning quality, we propose ReaScore, a task-adaptive metric that evaluates reasoning by automatically weighting task-relevant dimensions. Experiments on 14 leading MLLMs reveal a substantial gap between answers and reasoning, especially on challenging tasks. Further analysis shows that task-adaptive dimension selection and weighting better align evaluation results with expert judgments. Overall, our benchmark and metric support a more expert-aligned assessment of CCH understanding and offer a transferable reference for broader evaluations of cultural heritage. We publicly release the data, code, and evaluation scripts at https://github.com/ZevTsao/CulMind to facilitate reproducible research.

</details>


### [336] [Evaluating Document-Tuned Transformer Representations for Person-level Mental Health Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21622)
*Aaron Marker,Oscar Kjell,Vasudha Varadarajan,H. Andrew Schwartz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了基础Transformer和文档微调Transformer（句子Transformer）在心理健康和人格评估任务中的表现，发现文档微调模型在相关性上提升13.4%（p=.015），对文本扰动更鲁棒，且能更好捕捉不确定性语言特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人层面的心理评估需要聚合同一个体多条消息的语义，而文档级训练目标并非为此设计。现有研究缺乏对文档级微调表示（如句子Transformer）与传统基础Transformer在心理评估任务上的系统性比较。

Method: 在架构匹配的条件下，系统对比基础Transformer和文档微调Transformer（经对比学习文档级微调）。在两个纵向心理健康与心理数据集上，按层和整体进行评估，并进行稳健性分析（词删除、同义词替换、拼写错误、回译）及语言特征分析。

Result: 文档微调模型Pearson r提升13.4%（p=.015）；在四种扰动下保持更高准确性；语言特征分析显示文档微调嵌入中模糊限制语（如"usually"）更突出，而基础Transformer中 abundance性词汇（如"lot"）更突出。

Conclusion: 表示学习的选择影响心理健康预测效果，文档微调模型通常更擅长该任务，可能因其能更好捕捉不确定性表达。

Abstract: Person-level psychological assessment requires aggregating meaning across many messages from the same individual, a task that document-level training objectives were not explicitly designed for. We present a systematic, empirical comparison between architecturally matched traditional (a) base-transformers and (b) document-tuned-transformers (further contrastively fine-tuned at the document-level, sometimes referred to as "sentence transformers") under otherwise identical conditions. Comparing layer-wise and overall performance across two longitudinal mental health and psychological datasets, we find document-tuned models demonstrated a consistent improvement over base representations (increase in Pearson r of 13.4%, p=.015). Robustness analyses revealed document-tuned models remained more accurate under perturbations to word deletion, synonym replacement, typo injection, and back translation. Further, hedged language (e.g., `usually') was more characteristic of outcomes in document-tuned embeddings while abundance (e.g., `lot') was more characteristic of base-transformers, suggesting document-tuned models may better capture uncertainty. These results suggest representation choice impacts mental health prediction, document-tuned models often being more adept.

</details>


### [337] [CuratorKIT : Data Curation and Synthetic Data Generation for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21631)
*Soham Bhattacharjee,Karun Sharma,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu,Pratinav Seth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CuratorKIT是一个开源Python库，提供从数据摄取、去重、合成生成到质量过滤的完整后训练数据管线的统一框架，支持可审计的逐样本溯源链和结构化失败原因记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据整理工具将摄取、去重、合成生成和质量过滤视为独立阶段，导致难以审计管线决策或理解单个样本被拒绝的原因，缺乏统一的、可审计的端到端解决方案。

Method: 构建包含6种源格式读取器与自动schema检测、预处理卫生层（凭证/PII/有害内容）、8种LLM驱动的生成任务、3个互补质量门控（含精确溯源幻觉验证）、结构化自适应恢复机制、5种训练就绪导出格式的单一可配置管线，所有决策记录在仅追加的逐样本溯源链中。

Result: 支持100+LLM提供商（通过LiteLLM），提供Python API和YAML驱动的CLI，被拒绝样本带有结构化失败原因而非静默丢弃，可扩展至大规模生产环境。

Conclusion: CuratorKIT为需要可复现、可审计的大规模数据管线的从业者提供了完整的、端到端的数据整理解决方案，解决了现有工具碎片化的问题。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical part of post-training pipelines for large language models, yet existing tools often treat ingestion, deduplication, synthetic generation, and quality filtering as separate stages. This fragmentation makes it difficult to audit pipeline decisions or understand why individual samples are rejected. CuratorKIT is an open-source Python library that covers this full lifecycle in a single configurable pipeline. The framework is composed of six source format readers and automatic schema detection, a pre-generation data hygiene layer for credentials, PII, and toxic content, eight LLM-powered generation tasks, three complementary quality gates with provenance-exact hallucination verification, structured adaptive recovery, and five training-ready export formats compatible with TRL, Unsloth, and AlignTune. Every pipeline decision is recorded in an append-only per-sample provenance chain, and rejected samples carry structured failure reasons rather than being silently discarded. CuratorKIT supports 100+ LLM providers through LiteLLM, exposes both a Python API and a YAML-driven CLI, and is designed for practitioners who need reproducible, auditable data pipelines at scale .

</details>


### [338] [Behavioral and Representational Evidence of Binomial Ordering Preferences in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21645)
*Zhiqing Yang,Yilun Liu,Yunpu Ma,Volker Tresp,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在建模语言中梯度频率分布（以语言学二项式如"men and women"的语序偏好为例）方面的能力，发现LLMs虽能恢复主导性语序偏好，但无法准确捕捉精确的语料分布，并通过稀疏探测验证了偏好强度可在模型内部表征中被测量和操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs能够复现常规表达，但其对语言中梯度频率分布（即非二元对立的、连续的统计偏好）的建模能力尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将二项式语序形式化为分布对齐问题；构建包含8种语言600对二项式的多语言数据集；使用分类指标和分布指标，比较语料库偏好与6个开源LLM的语序预测概率；采用稀疏探测方法分析模型内部表征，并通过沿探测方向引导来操控模型的语序偏好分布。

Result: LLMs能较好地恢复语料库中主导性语序（尤其是强规约化二项式），但与精确的语料偏好分布对齐较差；偏好强度概念部分编码在模型的中后层，通过内部表征操控可改变模型的语序偏好分布。

Conclusion: 表面上的方向性语序偏好高估了LLMs捕捉语言统计细微差别的能力；模型的统计行为偏好可以通过内部表征进行机械性测量和操控。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can readily reproduce conventional expressions, yet their ability to model gradient frequency distributions remains underexplored. We investigate this using linguistic binomials, such as men and women, where both word permutations are grammatically valid but exhibit distinct, cross-linguistic variations in conventionality. We formalize binomial ordering as a distributional alignment problem, and construct a multilingual dataset of 600 binomial pairs across 8 languages. With categorical and distributional metrics, we measure and compare the corpus-derived preferences with model-induced ordering probabilities of 6 open-weight LLMs. While models often behaviorally recover the dominant corpus-preferred order, particularly for strongly conventionalized pairs, they align less well with the exact corpus preference distributions. This suggests that apparent directional order overstates how faithfully LLMs capture the statistical nuances of language use. Sparse probing verifies that the concept of preference strength is partially encoded among middle-to-late layers, and steering along probe-derived directions alters model-induced ordering distributions, demonstrating that the statistical behavioral preference of LLMs can be mechanistically measured and manipulated via internal representations.

</details>


### [339] [EvoEmbedding: Evolvable Representations for Long-Context Retrieval and Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21649)
*Chang Nie,Chaoyou Fu,Junlan Feng,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvoEmbedding是一种能够生成可演化表示的检索嵌入模型，通过维护持续更新的隐式记忆来处理动态、序列化的长上下文信息，在多项长上下文检索基准上超越大模型，并泛化到下游任务与Agent工作流中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型本质上是静态的：它们孤立地编码文本片段，忽略了周围的上下文和时间顺序，无法适应动态、序列化的长上下文场景中对连续状态追踪的需求。

Method: EvoEmbedding维护一个持续更新的隐式记忆，在序列化处理输入时结合原始内容共同生成可演化嵌入；构建EvoTrain-180K数据集联合优化隐式记忆与检索；引入记忆队列防止循环编码中的表示坍塌，以及分段批处理技术处理长度差异并加速训练3.8倍。

Result: 在长上下文检索基准上超越更大规模的专业模型（如Qwen3-Embedding-8B和KaLM-Embedding-Gemma3-12B）；在训练窗口10倍长的下游任务（如个性化）上表现良好；集成到朴素RAG管线中即超越专用的Agent记忆系统。

Conclusion: EvoEmbedding证明了结合动态隐式记忆的可演化嵌入在长上下文检索和Agent工作流中的有效性，为超越静态语义搜索提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing embedding models are inherently static: they encode text segments in isolation, ignoring their surrounding context and temporal order. This paper introduces EvoEmbedding, a novel embedding model that generates evolvable representations for retrieval. It is tailored for long-context scenarios, where information is dynamic, sequential, and requires continuous state tracking. Our design is simple: EvoEmbedding maintains a continuously updated latent memory as it sequentially processes inputs, and uses it alongside the raw content to jointly generate evolvable embeddings. Consequently, for the same query, our model adapts its representation to retrieve distinct targets based on the evolving context, going beyond static semantic search. To equip the model with this capability, we construct EvoTrain-180K, a diverse dataset for the joint optimization of latent memory and retrieval. Furthermore, we introduce a memory queue to prevent representation collapse during recurrent encoding, alongside segment-batching techniques that tackle significant length variance and accelerate training by 3.8$\times$. Extensive experiments show that our model not only outperforms larger-scale specialists (e.g., Qwen3-Embedding-8B and KaLM-Embedding-Gemma3-12B) across a range of long-context retrieval benchmarks, but also generalizes well to downstream tasks (e.g., personalization) with contexts 10$\times$ longer than its training window. Notably, EvoEmbedding seamlessly integrates into agentic workflows to boost performance. For instance, a naive RAG pipeline equipped with our model surpasses dedicated agentic memory systems. Project Page: https://clare-nie.github.io/EvoEmbedding.

</details>


### [340] [TACO: Task-Aware Column Description Generation Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21685)
*Ting Cai,Rakesh R. Menon,Yiru Chen,Zifan Liu,Yuan Tian,Fei Wu,Anudeep Chimakurthi,Prashanthi Ramamurthy,Sunav Choudhary,Kun Qian,Yunyao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TACO 是一个面向表格数据的任务感知型列描述自动生成框架，通过三步流水线（缩写扩展、描述生成、描述修订）解决现有 LLM 方法在缩写处理、幻觉和冗余方面的不足，在下游任务上性能提升高达 32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据集普遍存在列名缩写、领域术语晦涩、文档缺失等问题，而现有基于单次提示的 LLM 方法在列描述生成中存在缩写处理不一致、描述幻觉或不完整、冗余模糊等三个关键缺陷，亟需一个更鲁棒和任务感知的自动化方法。

Method: 提出 TACO 框架，包含三步流水线：(1) 缩写扩展，标准化列名；(2) 描述生成，利用 LLM 生成富含同义词和搜索关键词的初始语义描述；(3) 描述修订，通过模拟下游任务对生成描述进行优化。此外还探索了人在回路扩展，并发布了实体链接和模式丰富的新评估数据集。

Result: 在公开和专有数据集上的大量实验表明，TACO 在所有指标上一致优于现有方法，下游任务性能提升高达 32%。

Conclusion: TACO 通过任务感知的三步流水线有效解决了列描述生成中的关键挑战，显著提升了下游任务性能，验证了结合模拟下游任务反馈进行描述优化的有效性。

Abstract: Generating accurate and informative column descriptions (e.g. "membership status of customers" for the column name "cust_mem") is essential for a wide range of downstream NLP tasks on tabular data, including NL2SQL, table question answering, and entity linking. This problem arises in enterprises, domain sciences, government data portals, and so on. Despite its importance, most real-world datasets suffer from missing or cryptic documentation, often due to abbreviated column names or domain-specific jargon. Existing approaches largely rely on single-prompt large language models (LLMs), which struggle with three key issues: (i) inconsistent or incorrect handling of abbreviations, (ii) hallucinated or incomplete descriptions, and (iii) redundancy or vagueness that hinders downstream performance. We present TACO, a task-aware framework for automatic column description generation using LLMs. TACO introduces a three-step pipeline: (1) abbreviation expansion, which standardizes column names; (2) description generation, which produces initial semantic descriptions enriched with synonyms and search-oriented keywords; and (3) description revision, which refines these outputs using simulated downstream tasks. In addition, we investigate human-in-the-loop extensions and release new evaluation datasets for entity linking and schema enrichment. Extensive experiments across public and proprietary datasets show that TACO consistently outperforms existing methods, improving downstream task performance by up to 32%.

</details>


### [341] [Clinical Term Extraction using Open-Source Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21689)
*Noah Marchal,William E. Janes,Mihail Popescu,Xing Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了26种开源小语言模型在少样本提示下从非结构化的临床笔记中检测ALS相关术语的能力，发现正则表达式基线在总体性能上优于所有小语言模型，但部分模型在特定标签类别中表现突出，支持采用混合提取策略而非完全替代规则方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALS护理中的临床信息通常记录在非结构化的临床笔记中，难以进行下游分析。将非结构化的临床笔记内容提取为结构化格式对于后续分析和研究至关重要，传统规则方法存在局限性，而小语言模型提供了一种无需任务特定训练数据的替代方案。

Method: 使用26种开源小语言模型，采用少样本提示方法，检测17个ALS相关临床术语类别（包括功能评分、呼吸测量、药物等）。从JSON编码的出院小结中提取临床笔记内容，通过提示模板生成结构化JSON输出，并与正则表达式基线和TF-IDF基线进行比较，使用聚合、标签级别和人工验证的多标签分类指标进行评估。

Result: 正则表达式基线在总体微F1和汉明损失上优于所有小语言模型和TF-IDF基线；Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507是表现最佳的小语言模型；TF-IDF基线召回率高但精确率低；部分小语言模型精确率高但召回率低；Hammer2.1-7b在ALSFRS-R子评分检测中表现突出。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，当前小语言模型在ALS临床术语提取任务中尚无法完全替代规则方法，但可支持有针对性的混合提取工作流，根据不同标签类别选择最优方法。

Abstract: Clinical information for amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS) care documented in unstructured clinical notes limits downstream analysis without extraction into structured formats. Open-source small language models with few-shot prompting for detecting the presence of ALS-relevant clinical terms in patient documentation were evaluated without task-specific training data. The detection task targeted 17 categories spanning functional scores, respiratory measures, medications, and related clinical and non-clinical attributes. Clinical note content was normalized from JSON-encoded discharge summaries and processed with a prompt template having structured JSON outputs. We compared 26 open-source models using aggregate, label-level, and manual-validation multilabel classification metrics. Manual validation showed that a regex rule baseline had higher overall micro-F1 and lower Hamming loss than any single SLM or TF-IDF baseline, while Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 was the highest-performing SLM by micro-F1. Model rankings varied by metric and label category, with the TF-IDF baseline showing high recall but low precision, some SLMs showing higher precision but lower recall, and Hammer2.1-7b showing strong performance for ALSFRS-R subscore detection. These findings support targeted hybrid extraction workflows rather than replacement of existing rule-based methods.

</details>


### [342] [When Compression Helps and When It Hurts: Condition-Aware Analysis of Chain-of-Thought Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21704)
*Siyang Lyu,Zhijing Sun,Xinghao Chen,Tong Liu,Dawei Zhu,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Chain-of-Thought（CoT）蒸馏中的压缩问题，从重要性标准、重构级别和压缩预算三个维度进行消融实验，发现：(i) 重要性标准的效果严格受粒度控制，步骤级标准收敛于共同推理骨架，而词元级剪枝需要符号感知信号；(ii) 重构级别在不同领域效果相反，数学任务随结构破坏单调下降，通用任务中激进重写反而起到去噪作用；(iii) 训练时压缩不必然转化为推理时节省，Long-CoT学生会保留冗长习惯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT压缩方法（选择性剪枝和生成式重写）中关键因素相互纠缠，如粒度与重要性标准混淆、重构级别未被独立研究、压缩预算未跨域系统评估，缺乏统一的解耦分析框架。

Method: 将CoT压缩重新定义为三个维度：重要性标准（importance criterion）、重构级别（restructuring level）和压缩预算（compression budget）。在两类模型族（Math和General领域）、Long/Short-CoT机制下进行系统消融实验。

Result: (i) 步骤级剪枝标准收敛于共同推理骨架，词元级需符号感知信号；(ii) 数学任务对结构破坏敏感，通用任务中重写可去噪；(iii) 训练时压缩不保证推理时节省，Long-CoT学生模型仍保持冗长输出。

Conclusion: 提出了条件感知的压缩指南，指导根据部署上下文匹配压缩策略，强调不同领域和推理机制下需要差异化处理。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) distillation transfers multi-step reasoning from large reasoning models to smaller students, but verbose teacher traces inflate both training and inference cost. Existing CoT compression methods fall into two families, selective pruning and generative rewriting, yet prior studies have left key factors entangled: granularity is confounded with importance criteria in pruning, restructuring level is rarely isolated in rewriting, and compression budgets are not systematically evaluated across domains or regimes. We recast CoT compression along three dimensions: importance criterion, restructuring level, and compression budget. Sweeping these across two model families, Math and General domains, and Long-/Short-CoT regimes, we find that (i) importance criterion utility is strictly governed by granularity: step-level criteria converge on a shared reasoning backbone, while token-level pruning requires symbol-aware signals to preserve the logical core; (ii) restructuring level inverts across domains: Math degrades monotonically with structural disruption, while aggressive rewriting acts as a denoiser on General tasks; (iii) training-time compression does not necessarily translate to inference-time savings: Long-CoT students retain verbose habits despite concise supervision, making the training ratio an optimistic lower bound on deployment cost. These findings yield condition-aware guidelines for matching compression to deployment context.

</details>


### [343] [PrivacyAlign: Contextual Privacy Alignment for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21710)
*Manveer Singh Tamber,Abhay Puri,Marc-Etienne Brunet,Perouz Taslakian,Jimmy Lin,Spandana Gella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PrivacyAlign数据集（1350样本，3516条来自599名标注者的详细标注），以人类隐私判断为核心对AI智能体进行隐私对齐。通过标注条件奖励建模和基于人类标注的LLM评判，使智能体决策更符合人类隐私规范，在隐私对齐和现有基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体代表用户做决策时，隐私对齐至关重要——每次消息、帖子或工具调用都涉及关于分享什么、与谁分享、在什么条件下分享的情境判断。现有工作依赖不可靠的代理指标进行训练和评估，而隐私判断本质上依赖社会期望和规范，人类判断不仅标记隐私违规，更帮助定义何为违规。

Method: 1) 构建PrivacyAlign数据集：收集1350个样本，针对当前LLM实际泄露隐私的场景，由599名标注者提供3516条详细标注；2) 标注条件LLM评判：将人类标注和参考回复的解释作为条件输入LLM评判器，提升判断可靠性；3) 标注条件奖励建模：在强化学习阶段使用人类标注对新回复进行评分，训练小规模开源智能体。

Result: 标注条件LLM评判比无条件的评判更加可靠；使用标注条件奖励模型训练的小规模开源智能体在PrivacyAlign和现有智能体隐私基准上均取得显著提升，更好地与人类隐私规范对齐。

Conclusion: 将人类隐私判断置于AI智能体隐私对齐的核心是有效且必要的。PrivacyAlign数据集和标注条件方法为智能体隐私对齐提供了更可靠的训练和评估基础，证明了人类规范性判断在定义和检测隐私违规中的关键作用。

Abstract: AI agents acting on behalf of users are constantly making decisions, and for users to trust their agents, those decisions must align with what they actually want. Privacy is an important alignment problem for agents: every message, post, or tool call an agent makes is a contextual judgment about what is appropriate to share, with whom, and under which conditions. Because such judgments depend on social expectations and norms, human judgment does not merely label privacy violations but also helps define them. While existing work relies on unreliable proxies for both training and evaluation, we place human judgment at the center of agentic privacy alignment. We introduce PrivacyAlign, a dataset of 1,350 samples with 3,516 detailed annotations from 599 unique annotators across diverse scenarios where current LLMs actually leak, and use it to ground both alignment training and automated evaluation in human privacy norms. Building on these annotations, we first show that conditioning LLM judges on human annotations and explanations for reference responses to the same prompt makes their judgments more reliable. We then introduce annotation-conditioned reward modeling, which uses these annotations to score new responses during RL, and show that small open-weight agents trained with this reward better align with human privacy norms, with strong gains on PrivacyAlign and existing privacy benchmarks for agents.

</details>


### [344] [Leveraging LaBSE with Progressive Curriculum Learning for Multicultural Polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21718)
*Sachin Sundar,Sandeep Kumar,Mothish M*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用LaBSE嵌入的架构，用于SemEval-2026任务9的多语言、多文化在线极化现象检测，在低资源语言上提升了至多0.2 macro F1，并提供了Qwen模型族在检索式提示框架下的全面消融实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言和多文化环境下的在线极化现象检测面临数据稀缺的挑战，尤其是在低资源语言中，现有方法难以有效建模跨语言和跨文化群体间的敌意与对立。

Method: 提出一种基于LaBSE嵌入的架构（通常用于检索任务），利用其强大的跨语言表征能力实现跨语言学习；同时使用Qwen模型族进行检索式提示（retrieval-based prompting），并开展全面的消融实验评估不同编码器的性能。

Result: 在低资源语言上，相比基线方法提升至多0.2 macro F1分数。

Conclusion: LaBSE嵌入在跨语言学习任务中具有超出传统检索场景的潜力，检索式提示框架结合适当的编码器选择能有效缓解低资源语言的数据稀缺问题，为多语言极化检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Detecting online polarization remains a critical challenge, particularly in multilingual and multicultural contexts where intergroup hostility is prevalent. The problem is particularly challenging due to the data scarcity for these tasks in the low-resource languages. Identifying such phenomena has become an active area of research and is addressed in SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual, Multicultural Online Polarization Detection. To address this problem we propose an architecture that leverages LaBSE embeddings - an unconventional choice typically reserved for retrieval tasks, to obtain strong cross-lingual learning which enhances scores in low-resource language by a score up to 0.2 macro F1. Furthermore, we provide a comprehensive ablation study evaluating the performance of diverse encoder models in the Qwen model family within a retrieval-based prompting framework. Our code will be soon available at https://github.com/carrycurious/PolarMind.

</details>


### [345] [Denoising Iterative Self-Correction: Structured Verification Loops for Reliable LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21724)
*Shen Yin,David Ken,Joel Stremmel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Denoising Iterative Self-Correction (DISC)方法，通过将验证问题输出视为解中可能被破坏位置的噪声测量，迭代地进行验证-判断-纠正的多轮修正，并引入二元判断门控控制修正精度，在三个基准测试上优于Chain-of-Verification和Self-Refine方法，同时发现跨模型角色分配可以缓解自我确认偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的推理步骤流畅但常不正确，而简单的纠正方法可能破坏已正确的答案，需要一种既能修复错误又不会损害正确回答的精确校正方法。

Method: 提出DISC框架，将验证问题的输出视为噪声测量，通过多轮验证-判断-纠正（verification-judgment-correction）迭代过程逐步减少错误。引入二元判断门控（binary judgment gate）来控制修正精度，只有当判断认为有必要时才进行重写。评估使用改进-退化比（precision）和修复率（recall）两个配对诊断指标。

Result: 在BIG-Bench Mistake、HotpotQA、GPQA Diamond三个基准测试和四个模型上，DISC在精度-召回权衡上优于Chain-of-Verification和Self-Refine。在BIG-Bench Mistake上（Sonnet 4.5）达到81.6%准确率，每次退化的改进次数比Chain-of-Verification多13倍，比Self-Refine多5倍。在GPQA Diamond上发现能力下限，模型能识别证据矛盾但无法转化为纠正。跨模型角色分配可缓解自我确认偏差。

Conclusion: DISC是一种有效的测试时自我修正方法，通过迭代去噪策略和判断门控机制实现了高精度的错误修复；跨模型角色分配是缓解自我确认偏差的有效策略。

Abstract: Large language models produce fluent but often incorrect multi-step reasoning, and naive correction methods risk degrading already-correct answers. We introduce Denoising Iterative Self-Correction (DISC), a test-time procedure that treats verification question outputs as noisy measurements of where a solution may be corrupted. Using these signals, DISC progressively reduces errors across multiple verify-judge-correct passes, analogous to traditional iterative denoising. A binary judgment gate controls correction precision by blocking rewrites that would damage already-correct answers while the verifier and corrector together repair errors. We evaluate this trade-off using two paired diagnostics: an improvement-to-degradation ratio (precision) and a repair rate (recall). Across three benchmarks (BIG-Bench Mistake, HotpotQA, GPQA Diamond) and four models, DISC dominates Chain-of-Verification and Self-Refine on the precision-recall trade-off, reaching 81.6% accuracy with 13x more improvements per degradation than Chain-of-Verification and 5x more than Self-Refine on BIG-Bench Mistake (Sonnet~4.5). On GPQA Diamond, we identify a capability floor below which judges acknowledge contradictions in evidence but cannot translate that recognition into a correction. We further show that cross-model role allocation -- assigning verification and judgment to a model different from the generator -- mitigates self-confirmation bias.

</details>


### [346] [CalVerT: Augmenting Agents with Calibrated Verifier Telemetry Improves Action and Learning in Knowledge-Intensive Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21777)
*Ashwin Vinod,Ying Ding,Elias Stengel-Eskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出校准验证器遥测（CalVerT），通过向LLM智能体状态添加校准的自信心分数和基础验证器分数，帮助智能体在知识密集型问答中更全面地感知状态空间，从而减少过早承诺未支持答案和过度检索两种失败模式，在无需训练和基于训练的场景中均能提升F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在知识密集型问答中因无法判断当前答案是否不确定、未得到支持或已完成，导致两种失败：过早给出自信但无依据的答案（损害准确性），以及证据已足够时过度检索（浪费计算资源）。需为智能体提供更完整的状态空间感知。

Method: 提出CalVerT框架，为智能体状态额外注入两种遥测信号：校准的自信心分数（反映模型对答案的确信程度）和基础验证器分数（评估答案是否得到上下文支持）。在无需训练的场景中直接增强现有QA框架；在训练场景中，通过强化学习时简单地将这些遥测信息加入智能体状态进行训练。

Result: 在四个QA基准上，CalVerT通过触发在智能体过度依赖参数化知识时的检索、减少上下文已足够时的冗余检索，提升了F1分数。在无需训练和基于训练的设定下均观察到改进效果。

Conclusion: CalVerT通过简单的状态增强即可有效改善LLM智能体在知识密集型问答中的检索-推理决策质量，兼具训练无关和训练增强两种适用场景，是一种轻量且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: LLM agents in knowledge intensive question answering take retrieval and reasoning actions with incomplete knowledge about whether their current answer is uncertain, unsupported, or already complete. This produces two failure modes: committing to confident but unsupported answers, which hurts accuracy, and over-retrieving when the evidence in hand already suffices, resulting in wasted compute. To give agents a more complete picture of the state space they are operating in, we introduce calibrated verifier telemetry (CalVerT), which augments the agent's state with additional telemetry: a calibrated self-confidence score and a grounding verifier score. We show that CalVerT can improve agents in both training-free and training-based settings. On four QA benchmarks, we find that CalVerT raises F1 by triggering retrieval in cases where agents over-rely on parametric knowledge, while cutting redundant retrieval in cases where agents have sufficient context to answer. We show that CalVerT can augment existing QA frameworks without training. Moreover, CalVerT also improves trained systems: by simply augmenting an agent's state with telemetry, we observe improvements after reinforcement learning, as compared to an agent with identical training but no CalVerT telemetry.

</details>


### [347] [When to Plan, When to Polish: Noise Level as a Granularity Axis for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21802)
*Peihong Li,Yuanjie Shi,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出噪声依赖粒度控制（NDGC）方法，通过将噪声水平作为粒度线索，使扩散LM在去噪过程中自动从粗粒度（词元组）过渡到细粒度（单个词元），实现类似规划的先粗后细生成，无需显式规划器或分层架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准词元级扩散语言模型在去噪全程保持词元粒度的训练和推理，高噪声阶段只能产生零散局部片段，难以形成早期粗粒度结构，不利于需要规划能力的文本生成。

Method: 提出NDGC方法，利用噪声水平作为粒度控制信号：高噪声步骤使用连贯词元组（粗粒度）进行训练和推理，支持早期语义承诺；低噪声步骤恢复词元级（细粒度）精修，形成从粗到细的单层扩散过程。

Result: 在控制测试、消融实验和WritingPrompts数据集上，NDGC实现了更早的骨架形成、更有序的恢复过程和更健康的生成输出。

Conclusion: NDGC通过噪声水平驱动的粒度控制，在单层扩散中实现了类似规划的粗到细生成，无需额外规划器或分层架构，证明了噪声本身可作为粒度线索有效指导扩散式文本生成。

Abstract: Standard tokenwise diffusion LMs keep training corruption and inference commitment at token granularity throughout denoising. At high noise, this leaves scattered local fragments rather than coherent evidence, making it hard to form early coarse structure, exactly what planning-sensitive generation requires. Hierarchical planning methods add coarse stages to separate planning from wording, but they need extra planners, block latents, or two stage designs. We propose Noise Dependent Granularity Control (NDGC), a single-level diffusion method that uses the noise level as a granularity cue. NDGC aligns training exposure and inference commitment with denoising progress. High noise steps use coherent token groups to support early meaning commitment, while low noise steps return to token level refinement. This creates planning like coarse to fine denoising without an explicit planner or hierarchical architecture. Across controlled tests, ablations, and WritingPrompts, NDGC shows earlier skeleton formation, better ordered recovery, and healthier outputs.

</details>


### [348] [Test-Time Training with Next-Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21803)
*Xuan Ouyang,Zefan Cai,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TTT-NTP方法，利用预训练语言模型自身的下一个token预测信号作为测试时训练的监督信号，通过存储下一个位置的上下文隐藏状态来实现快速权重自适应。该方法无需重新设计骨干网络，在RULER Full-13和LongBench-v2基准测试上，对Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B-v0.3、Qwen3系列等多种模型均取得一致提升，同时保持常识和知识性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时训练方法要么需要重新设计模型架构，不适用于已发布的预训练大语言模型检查点；要么虽然支持就地快速权重适应，但存储的监督信号与模型自监督的下一token预测目标没有直接联系。本文旨在解决测试时每次写入应存储什么这一核心问题，使测试时训练与预训练任务的因果计算一致。

Method: TTT-NTP是一种即插即用的快速权重适应方法。其核心思想是利用模型自身下一个位置上下文隐藏状态的线性投影作为值目标来监督更新，使每个局部写入过程遵循与下一token预测相同的因果计算路径。该方法直接使用预训练模型已有的下一token预测信号，无需额外学习局部值代理。

Result: 在RULER Full-13上（平均4k、8k、16k、32k上下文长度），TTT-NTP是唯一在四种模型（Llama-3.1-8B +3.9、Mistral-7B-v0.3 +3.0、Qwen3-4B +4.1、Qwen3-0.6B +2.9）上均一致提升的方法。在LongBench-v2长文档问答上，Llama-3.1-8B提升+5.6，Mistral-7B-v0.3提升+3.7，且保持常识和知识性能。

Conclusion: TTT-NTP通过将测试时训练的监督信号直接对齐到预训练模型自身的下一token预测目标，实现了对多种预训练大语言模型的有效长上下文适应，证明了自监督的下一token预测信号在测试时训练中的有效性，为无需重新训练的LLM自适应提供了简洁而通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Next-token prediction is the self-supervised signal that trains language models, and every observed prompt token provides the same signal at test time. We study whether this signal can define the inner-loop objective for test-time training (TTT) in pretrained long-context language models. Many TTT architectures require models to be trained with test-time adaptation in mind, limiting their direct applicability to released LLM checkpoints. While recent in-place TTT methods make fast-weight adaptation possible for pretrained LLMs without redesigning the backbone, they leave a central question unresolved: what should each test-time write store? Existing recipes train the fast weight to match a learned local value proxy but they are not directly tied to the self-supervised next-token prediction signal. We introduce Test-Time Training with Next-Token Prediction (TTT-NTP), a drop-in fast-weight adaptation method for pretrained LLMs that instead supervises updates using the model's own next contextual hidden state. This makes each local write follow the same causal computation that supports next-token prediction: the value target is a pointwise linear projection of a single next-position contextual state. On RULER Full-13 (averaged over 4k, 8k, 16k, and 32k context lengths), TTT-NTP is the only method that consistently improves the released backbone across four models spanning three families and a 0.6--8B size range: Llama-3.1-8B (+3.9), Mistral-7B-v0.3 (+3.0), and the Qwen3 series (Qwen3-4B +4.1, Qwen3-0.6B +2.9). On the real-world LongBench-v2 long-document QA benchmark, TTT-NTP improves over the base model on both Llama-3.1-8B (+5.6) and Mistral-7B-v0.3 (+3.7), while preserving commonsense and knowledge performance.

</details>


### [349] [Fixed RAG Compression Collapses Measured Reader Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21807)
*Sugam Panthi,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了检索增强生成（RAG）压缩研究中的一个根本性假设错误：压缩效果并非评估中性，固定压缩在提升平均准确率的同时，会掩盖读者模型升级的真实效果，甚至反转模型排名。通过对20个读者、10个领域-方法设置、4个QA基准和1个摘要基准的广泛实验，发现压缩收益随读者基线的提升而降低（9/10设置显著，p<0.05）。通用摘要压缩在LongMemEval-S上翻转了31%的成对模型排名，固定压缩器隐藏了Qwen 7B到GPT-4.1-mini的80%原始提升。这一悖论源于两种相反作用力：压缩通过去除噪声拯救弱读者，同时通过删除细节伤害强读者。该模式在结构化编译、通用摘要、三种训练压缩器家族、查询聚焦摘要以及9篇已发表压缩论文的外部审计中均一致出现。作者发布ragscale工具包，基于177,000行级别的压缩转换数据，使任何压缩论文可在一天内用三个读者完成读者规模审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG压缩研究通常仅在一到三个读者上评估压缩器，并隐含假设压缩后的证据层是评估中性的。作者质疑这一假设，旨在揭示固定压缩对读者评估的隐藏偏差——即压缩在提升平均准确率的同时，会掩盖读者升级的真实增益并扭曲模型排名。

Method: 在20个读者、10个领域-方法设置、4个QA基准和1个摘要基准上系统评估压缩效果；分析压缩收益与读者基线准确率的关系；通过两种相反作用力（噪声去除与细节删除）解释悖论；对结构化编译、通用摘要、三种训练压缩器、查询聚焦摘要及9篇已发表论文进行外部审计；开发ragscale工具包（基于177,000行级别压缩转换数据）以支持快速审计。

Result: 固定压缩在提升平均准确率的同时，会隐藏读者升级的真实效果（如Qwen 7B到GPT-4.1-mini的80%增益被隐藏），并反转模型排名（31%的成对排名在LongMemEval-S上翻转）。压缩收益与读者基线显著负相关（9/10设置p<0.05）。该模式在多种压缩方法和已发表论文中一致出现。

Conclusion: RAG压缩研究必须放弃"压缩评估中性"的错误假设，采用多读者评估以避免偏差。ragscale工具包提供了一种高效方式，使任何压缩论文可在一天内用三个读者完成读者规模审计。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) compression papers often evaluate a compressor on one to three readers and treat the compressed evidence layer as evaluation-neutral. We show this assumption is false: fixed compression can raise average accuracy while hiding reader upgrades and reversing model rankings. Across 20 readers and ten domain-method settings over four QA benchmarks and one summarization benchmark, compression gain decreases with reader baseline (nine of ten settings significant, p < 0.05). Generic summarization flips 31% of pairwise model rankings on LongMemEval-S, and a fixed HotpotQA compressor hides 80% of the raw upgrade from Qwen 7B to GPT-4.1-mini. Two opposing forces explain this paradox: compression rescues weak readers by removing noise they cannot filter, and harms strong readers by dropping details they would have used. The pattern appears across structured compilation, generic summarization, three trained compressor families, query-focused summarization, and an external audit of nine published compression papers. We release ragscale, a toolkit built on 177,000 row-level compression transitions, so any compression paper can audit reader scaling with three readers in one day.

</details>


### [350] [Inverse Turing Bench: Evaluating Language Models as Judges of Human vs. AI Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21844)
*William Hager,Ishika Rathi,Masum Hasan,Cameron Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了Inverse Turing Bench基准测试，用于评估模型区分人类与AI在多轮对话中的能力。测试发现GPTZero、Claude Opus-4.6和GPT-5.5准确率最高（89.41%、77.92%、75.94%），统计方法存在语义盲点，语义方法易受角色提示影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统融入在线空间，区分对话中的人类与AI变得越来越重要，但目前缺乏相应的评估基准。

Method: 构建了配对对话转录本数据集（一组为双人对话，另一组为人机对话），要求模型识别哪一组是纯人类对话。评估了多种模型在该基准上的表现。

Result: GPTZero、Claude Opus-4.6和GPT-5.5准确率最高，分别为89.41%、77.92%和75.94%。统计方法存在语义盲点，语义方法易受角色提示攻击。

Conclusion: 该基准可作为LLM心智理论的探针，人机区分能力对AI系统至关重要。

Abstract: As AI systems integrate into online spaces, differentiating them from humans in conversations is increasingly important. We present Inverse Turing Bench, a benchmark that evaluates LLMs and other models on their ability to differentiate humans and AI in multi-turn text. The benchmark provides a collection of paired dialogue transcripts, wherein one dialogue is between two humans and the other is between a human and an AI. The task is to correctly identify which dialogue is human-only vs. human-AI. We evaluated a preliminary set of models against this benchmark, and found that GPTZero, Claude Opus-4.6, and GPT-5.5 achieve the highest accuracy: 89.41%, 77.92%, and 75.94% respectively. Our results suggest that statistical approaches to detection have semantic blind spots, but semantic approaches are susceptible to persona-prompting. Our work speaks to the Inverse Turing Test as a probe of LLM theory of mind, and motivates human-AI differentiation as a critical capability for AI systems. Our live benchmark can be found at https://huggingface.co/spaces/roc-hci/Inverse-Turing-Bench-Leaderboard (anonymity preserved).

</details>


### [351] [Keyless Attention: Value-Space Routing and Value-Only Caching for Efficient Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21848)
*Xin Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Keyless Attention机制，通过消除Key投影，仅使用Query和Value构建Value-Only Cache，将KV缓存减少50%的同时匹配或超越标准注意力的性能。进一步提出Depth-m注意力分解理论，Keyless Attention是其中的m=3特例，通过值空间路由矩阵替代Key投影，实现了路由与检索的耦合。在5个模型和4种架构上的实验表明，该方法在4/5的模型上困惑度匹敌或超越标准注意力，在零样本评估中4/5的常识推理基准上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制中的KV缓存随着序列长度增长而线性增加，成为长序列推理的关键瓶颈。作者旨在消除Key投影以减小KV缓存大小，同时保持或提升注意力机制的表达能力。此外，作者希望从理论角度统一理解标准注意力和Keyless Attention，提出注意力双线性形式的深度分解框架。

Method: 1) 提出Keyless Attention：完全移除Key投影，仅保留Query和Value投影，形成Value-Only Cache，KV缓存减少50%。2) 提出Depth-m注意力分解理论框架：将标准注意力视为深度为2的分解，Keyless Attention为深度为3的特例，引入值空间路由矩阵替代Key投影，实现路由与检索的耦合。3) 在5个模型（GPT-2 280M/557M、Pythia 410M、Qwen2 1.5B、Llama 3.2 1B）上进行实验验证。

Result: 1) 在4/5的模型上困惑度匹敌或超越标准QKV注意力。2) 在GPT-2 557M的零样本评估中，4/5的常识推理基准上优于标准注意力。3) 实现50%的KV缓存减少，同时解码吞吐量匹敌或超越标准注意力。

Conclusion: Keyless Attention通过消除Key投影实现了KV缓存减半和效率提升，同时通过Depth-m注意力分解理论提供了统一的理论框架。实验证明该方法在多数模型和任务上不损失性能甚至有所提升，为大语言模型的高效推理提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We propose Keyless Attention, an attention mechanism that eliminates the key projection entirely, operating over queries and values only. This yields a Value-Only Cache that reduces KV cache memory and access overhead by exactly 50% over standard attention, while matching or exceeding standard attention's decode throughput. Beyond efficiency, we introduce Depth-$m$ Attention Factorization: standard attention computes a depth-2 factorization of the attention bilinear form, while Keyless Attention realizes a depth-$m$ instance of this family. At m=3, Keyless Attention matches the projection matrix count of standard attention via a value-space routing matrix that replaces the key projection and introduces a coupling between routing and retrieval. Experiments across five models and four architectures (GPT-2 280M, GPT-2 557M, Pythia 410M, Qwen2 1.5B, and Llama 3.2 1B) show that Keyless Attention matches or outperforms standard QKV attention on perplexity in 4 out of 5 models. On downstream zero-shot evaluation (GPT-2 557M), Keyless Attention outperforms on 4 out of 5 commonsense reasoning benchmarks, while achieving 50% KV cache reduction throughout.

</details>


### [352] [TALAS: Teacher-Anchored Layer Alignment with Adaptive Sharpness-Aware Minimization for Embedding Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21851)
*Quoc Phong Dao,Hoang Son Nguyen,Pham Khanh Chi,Linh Ngo Van,Nguyen Thi Ngoc Diep,Thien Huu Nguyen,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TALAS提出了一种统一的KD框架，通过教师锚定层对齐、层对齐自蒸馏和自适应锐度感知最小化，在降低计算开销的同时提升了学生模型的句子嵌入性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法强制学生严格模仿教师的句子嵌入或内部特征，导致计算成本高昂且因能力差距导致性能次优。

Method: 1) 教师锚定机制：仅将教师最终句子嵌入蒸馏到学生上层；2) 层对齐自蒸馏：利用嵌入空间的几何关系约束，将知识自上而下传播到低层；3) 自适应锐度感知最小化(ASAM)：引导学生走向平坦最小值，防止记忆逐点教师噪声。

Result: 在标准句子嵌入基准上，TALAS持续优于强蒸馏基线，同时在计算成本和显存占用方面实现了更优的训练效率。

Conclusion: 通过层级对齐与鲁棒优化的协同，TALAS有效缓解了能力差距问题，兼顾了性能与训练效率。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) has established itself as a pivotal technique for compressing large pre-trained language models. However, existing methods that force a student to strictly mimic the teacher's sentence embeddings or internal features often incur prohibitive computational costs and yield suboptimal performance due to the inherent capacity gap. To address these challenges, we propose TALAS (Teacher-Anchored Layer Alignment with Sharpness-aware minimization), a unified framework that synergizes hierarchical (multi-layer) alignment with robust optimization. First, we introduce a Teacher-Anchored mechanism that selectively distills final sentence embeddings only into the student's upper layers, thereby reducing overhead while respecting capacity constraints. Second, we bridge the semantic gap in lower layers via Layer-Aligned Self-Distillation, which propagates knowledge top-down using internal geometric relational constraints in the embedding space. Finally, to prevent the student from memorizing point-wise teacher noise, we integrate Adaptive Sharpness-Aware Minimization (ASAM) into the training objective, guiding the model towards flat minima for enhanced generalization. Empirical results on standard sentence embedding benchmarks demonstrate that TALAS consistently outperforms strong distillation baselines while achieving superior training efficiency in terms of computational cost and memory footprint.

</details>


### [353] [The Language-Energy Divide: Measuring Energy Costs of Multilingual LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21869)
*Naihao Deng,Alissa Shen,Yiming Feng,Joan Nwatu,Jae-Won Chung,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Yulong Chen,Rada Mihalcea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多语言大模型推理时的能耗差异，发现不同语言之间每token能耗相差高达8.3倍，完成相同任务的总能耗从英语的17.6 kJ到普什图语的3,147 kJ，差距达179倍。这种"能源不平等"由复杂脚本的高token成本和低资源语言需生成更多token共同导致，且高能耗语言往往任务准确率更低。作者建议将能源效率作为评估大模型的一个核心维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多语言场景中部署日益普遍，但不同语言在推理时的能源消耗差异尚未得到系统研究，作者希望揭示这种差异的存在、成因及其影响。

Method: 使用ML.Energy框架(Chung et al., 2026)对多种语言的推理能耗进行系统测量和比较分析，涵盖不同模型、硬件和任务设置。

Result: 每输出token能耗跨语言差异高达8.3倍；完成固定请求集的总能耗从英语的17.6 kJ到普什图语的3,147 kJ，差距达179倍；低资源语言面临"高能耗+低准确率"的双重惩罚；该能源鸿沟在不同模型、硬件和任务中普遍存在。

Conclusion: 多语言LLM部署中存在系统性的能源不平等，建议将能源效率作为评估大模型的一级指标，在报告清单和模型卡中纳入能耗信息，并采取部署端缓解措施以提升能源效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in multilingual settings, yet the energy costs of serving these models across different languages remain poorly understood. We present a systematic study of inference energy consumption across languages with ML.Energy framework (Chung et al., 2026). We find striking disparities: energy consumption per output token varies by up to 8.3 times across languages, while total energy for a fixed set of requests varies by up to 179 times between the cheapest (English, 17.6 kJ) and the most expensive (Pashto, 3,147 kJ) languages. Our analysis shows that this disparity is driven by two compounding factors: (1) higher per-token energy costs for languages using complex or rare scripts, and (2) more tokens generated for low-resource languages. Moreover, we find a double cost + performance penalty: languages with the highest energy footprints also tend to achieve the lowest task accuracy. We reveal that the energy divide persists across models, hardware, and tasks, suggesting a systemic energy inequity in multilingual LLM deployment. Finally, we recommend that the community treat energy as a first-class evaluation axis, extend reporting checklists and model cards to include it, and adopt deployment-side mitigations for better energy efficiency.

</details>


### [354] [Scaling Performance and Low-Resource Annotation with Many-Shot In-Context Learning for Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21890)
*Qi Zhang,Fangping Lan,Cornelia Caragea,Longin Jan Latecki,Eduard Dragut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大规模上下文学习（many-shot ICL）在命名实体识别（NER）中的应用，发现使用数百个示例作为上下文可以让LLM匹配甚至超越全监督BERT模型，并且可以用约百个人工标注示例生成高质量标注数据，使低资源NER上的BERT微调F1提升约10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLM在NER等结构化任务上仍落后于微调BERT，且ICL用于NER的研究多停留在小样本设置，大规模演示（数百个示例）的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在多个领域评估多种LLM，使用数百个ICL示例进行NER，并探索将many-shot ICL作为数据标注框架，利用约百个人工标注示例生成数据用于微调BERT。

Result: (1) 扩展到数百个上下文示例使LLM能够匹配或超越全监督BERT模型的性能；(2) 使用约百个人工标注示例作为演示，many-shot上下文标注可生成高质量标注数据，用于微调BERT时在低资源NER上比现有SOTA方法提升约10%绝对F1。

Conclusion: 大规模上下文学习在NER任务中具有显著潜力，既能直接提升LLM性能，也能作为高效的数据标注框架辅助低资源场景。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) with large language models (LLMs) has emerged as a powerful alternative to fine-tuning for Named Entity Recognition (NER), achieving strong performance with minimal annotation and no additional training. However, prior work has shown that despite their adaptability, LLMs still lag behind fully supervised models such as fine-tuned BERT in structured tasks like NER. While existing studies on ICL for NER have mainly explored few-shot settings, the potential of scaling to hundreds of demonstrations has not been thoroughly investigated. To address this gap, we conduct a comprehensive investigation of many-shot ICL for NER and further explore its effectiveness in annotating and refining data for low-resource NER tasks. Specifically, we evaluate various LLMs across multiple domains using hundreds of ICL examples and then assess the feasibility of using many-shot ICL as a data annotation framework. Our experiments demonstrate that: (1) scaling to hundreds of in-context examples enables LLMs to match or even surpass the performance of fully supervised BERT models; and (2) using about one hundred human-labeled examples as demonstrations, many-shot in-context annotation can generate high-quality labeled data, leading to approximately 10% absolute F1 improvement over existing state-of-the-art approaches when used to fine-tune BERT on low-resource NER.

</details>


### [355] [Olfactory-Inspired Sparse Combinatorial Coding for Low-Resource Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21895)
*Bhushan Deshpande*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文受生物嗅觉系统中受体-肾小球稀疏组合编码机制的启发，提出了一种新颖的嗅觉瓶颈架构（receptor-glomerular bottleneck），将其插入标准词嵌入与BiLSTM-CRF序列模型之间，用于低资源命名实体识别任务。实验表明，该架构在完全从头训练、无预训练嵌入的条件下，尤其在数据极度稀缺（如1k句）时，能有效提升F1分数，充当强大的正则化器。在孟加拉语和泰卢固语等低资源语言上取得了显著改进，并观察到受体层自然涌现出稀疏特化编码模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言NER面临标注数据稀缺和无高质量预训练嵌入的双重困境；生物嗅觉系统通过受体-肾小球稀疏组合编码实现鲁棒表征学习，为在不确定条件下学习表示提供了启发性的神经计算范式。

Method: 提出受体-肾小球瓶颈架构，模拟嗅觉系统的稀疏组合编码机制，将其插入标准token嵌入与BiLSTM-CRF模型之间，在无预训练嵌入条件下从零训练，在6种多语言数据集上进行评估。

Result: 在1k句低资源控制条件下，至少一种嗅觉构型在所有6个数据集上取得最高平均F1；孟加拉语提升+6.23% F1（vs标准基线）和+8.47% F1（vs最佳控制基线）；泰卢固语在全量数据下提升+4.43% F1；受体层自然涌现稀疏特化。

Conclusion: 源自嗅觉网络的稀疏结构化编码在表示需从有限或含噪监督中学习时，是一种有效的归纳偏置和正则化手段。

Abstract: Named Entity Recognition (NER) in low-resource languages suffers from limited supervision and a lack of high-quality pretrained embeddings. Biological olfaction, which relies on sparse combinatorial coding through receptor and glomerular organization, offers a compelling paradigm for learning robust representations under uncertainty. In this paper, we introduce a receptor-glomerular bottleneck - a novel, biologically-inspired olfactory architecture - between standard token embeddings and a BiLSTM-CRF sequence model. We evaluate our architecture across six multilingual datasets trained entirely from scratch (without pre-trained embeddings) under varied data-scale conditions, including a strict 1k-sentence low-resource control. Our results demonstrate that introducing a representation bottleneck yields F1 score improvements under severe data scarcity, primarily by acting as a powerful regularizer. Under the 1k capped training condition, at least one olfactory-inspired configuration achieves the highest mean F1 score across all six datasets. While these improvements represent near-ties with generic bottleneck controls for most languages, the olfactory architecture provides a significant advantage in languages like Bangla (+6.23% F1 over standard baseline and +8.47% F1 over the best control baseline) where generic bottlenecks degrade performance. We also observe improvements in the ultra-low-resource Telugu setting (+4.43% F1) at full-scale, and find that sparse specialization naturally emerges within the receptor layer. Our findings suggest that structured sparse coding inspired by olfactory networks serves as an effective inductive bias and regularizer when representations must be learned from limited or noisy supervision.

</details>


### [356] [Which Review Aspect Has a Greater Impact on the Duration of Open Peer Review in Multiple Rounds? -- Evidence from Nature Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21904)
*Haomin Zhou,Ruxue Han,Jiangtao Zhong,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过提取同行评审报告中的细粒度方面并分析其情感与评审时长的关系，发现评审情感与评审时长呈弱负相关，其中"评价与结果"和"影响与研究价值"方面的情感与评审时长关联较强，且这种关系在不同评审轮次中存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审对学术出版至关重要，但不断增加的投稿量给审稿人和编辑带来巨大压力。本研究旨在探究对特定评审方面的情感与同行评审时长之间的关系，以及这种关系在不同学科和评审轮次间的差异，以支持针对性稿件修改并提高评审效率。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：首先从同行评审报告中提取细粒度方面，并使用情感分类模型确定每个方面的情感；其次分析方面级情感与同行评审时长之间的相关性，并计算不同评审轮次的情感得分以检验这些关系是否随轮次变化。

Result: 评审情感与评审时长存在弱但统计显著的负相关；"评价与结果"和"影响与研究价值"方面的情感与评审时长相关性较强；方面级情感与评审时长的关系在不同评审轮次间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 本研究将同行评审报告的文本内容与评审过程的时间特征联系起来，通过识别与评审时长更相关的评审方面，为作者优先排序修订、帮助审稿人和编辑提高评审效率提供依据，有助于减轻同行评审负担并加速学术交流和知识传播。

Abstract: Purpose: Peer review is essential to scientific publishing, but increasing submission volumes have placed growing pressure on reviewers and editors. This study examines the relationship between sentiment toward specific review aspects and peer review duration. It also investigates how this relationship varies across disciplines and review rounds, with the aim of supporting targeted manuscript revision and improving review efficiency.
  Design/methodology/approach: We adopt a two-stage approach. First, fine-grained aspects are extracted from peer review reports, and a sentiment classification model is used to determine the sentiment associated with each aspect. Second, correlations between aspect-level sentiment and peer review duration are analyzed. Sentiment scores are also calculated for different review rounds to determine whether these relationships change over successive rounds.
  Findings: Review sentiment has a weak but statistically significant negative correlation with peer review duration, indicating that more positive reviews tend to be associated with shorter review periods. Aspects concerning Evaluation and Results and Impact and Research Value show relatively stronger correlations with review duration. The relationships between aspect-level sentiment and review duration also differ significantly across review rounds.
  Originality/value: This study connects the textual content of peer review reports with the temporal characteristics of the review process. By identifying review aspects that are more closely associated with review duration, it provides evidence that may help authors prioritize revisions and assist reviewers and editors in improving review efficiency. The findings contribute to reducing the burden of peer review and accelerating scholarly communication and knowledge dissemination.

</details>


### [357] [Deeper is Not Always Better: Mitigating the Alignment Tax via Confident Layer Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21906)
*Xuanming Zhang,Sining Zhoubian,Yuxuan Chen,Tianyi Tang,An Yang,Sean Du,Chujie Zheng,Fei Huang,Dayiheng Liu,Gao Huang,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM解码时存在"猜测-精炼-扰动"动态：早期层粗略猜测，中间层精炼推理语义，最终层可能扰动预测结果。提出Confident Decoding，通过熵引导的保守后向搜索动态选择最可靠的近最终层，在零内存开销和<2%延迟增加下，在GPQA-Diamond等推理基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"深层表示=更可靠预测"的传统假设，揭示最终层可能存在扰动问题，并寻求一种无需训练的解码策略来绕过这种扰动。

Method: 提出Confident Decoding，一种基于熵引导的保守后向搜索策略，动态选择最可靠的近最终层进行解码。将层选择建模为最优停止问题，在有限投影噪声和主导对齐扰动假设下，给出理论保证。

Result: 在密集和MoE架构的LLM上，GPQA-Diamond、Omni-MATH、HLE等推理基准测试中取得一致改进，零内存开销，延迟增加小于2%。

Conclusion: 动态绕过最终层的扰动可以释放对齐LLM更强的推理能力。

Abstract: Autoregressive generation in large language models (LLMs) conventionally decodes from the final layer, assuming that deeper representations yield more reliable next-token predictions. We revisit this assumption by revealing a recurring Guess-Refine-Perturb dynamic: early layers form coarse guesses, intermediate layers refine reasoning-relevant semantics, and final layers can perturb these refined predictions toward generic or alignment-preferred tokens. We introduce Confident Decoding, a training-free decoding strategy that dynamically selects the most reliable near-final layer through entropy-guided conservative backward search. We further provide a theoretical formulation of layer selection as an optimal stopping problem, showing that under bounded projection noise and dominant late-stage alignment perturbation, our search rule filters perturbation while bounding the loss relative to the oracle refinement layer. Experiments across dense and Mixture-of-Experts LLMs demonstrate consistent gains on challenging reasoning benchmarks, including GPQA-Diamond, Omni-MATH, and HLE, with zero memory overhead and less than 2% latency increase. These results suggest dynamically bypassing final-layer perturbations can unlock stronger reasoning behavior from aligned LLMs.

</details>


### [358] [Pre-Generation Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models via Soft-Target Attention Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21917)
*Amina Miftakhova,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在生成前检测幻觉风险的新方法，通过软目标监督（基于随机采样输出的经验答案错误率）和注意力探针技术，在三个问答基准和五个模型上优于传统的二元分类和线性探针方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将幻觉检测视为对单个解码输出的二元分类问题，但这种方法未能充分利用模型的不确定性信息。作者希望将问题重新定义为风险估计，从而更准确地评估模型在给定提示下的错误概率，并避免生成完整输出所需的计算成本。

Method: 1. 将幻觉风险检测重新表述为风险估计问题，而非二元分类。2. 提出软目标监督信号：基于随机采样输出的经验答案错误率，并证明该估计量是模型在采样分布下每个提示错误概率的唯一无偏最小方差估计量。3. 将注意力探针（attention probing）适配到预生成设置，使检测器能够选择性聚合与幻觉相关的提示表征。

Result: 在三个问答基准和五个模型上，注意力探针在短答案任务上优于线性探针。用软目标监督替代二元标签进一步且持续地提升了检测质量。

Conclusion: 将幻觉检测重新定义为风险估计问题，并结合软目标监督和注意力探针，能够更有效地在生成前评估模型的幻觉风险，提升检测性能，同时避免完整解码的计算开销。

Abstract: Detecting hallucination risk before generation enables abstention, retrieval augmentation, and routing decisions without incurring the cost of decoding.
  While prior work has shown that such risk can be estimated from a model's internal representations, existing approaches treat this as binary classification over a single decoded output. We instead formulate it as a risk-estimation problem. Under this formulation, we introduce soft-target supervision based on the empirical answer error rate over stochastically sampled outputs - an estimator we prove to be the unique unbiased minimum-variance estimator of the model's per-prompt error probability under its sampling distribution.
  We further adapt attention probing to the pre-generation setting, enabling the detector to selectively aggregate hallucination-relevant prompt representations. Across three question-answering benchmarks and five models, attention probing outperforms linear probing on short-answer tasks. Replacing binary labels with soft-target supervision further and consistently improves detection quality.

</details>


### [359] [MindTailor: Personalized Emotional Support via Post History-Grounded Case Formulation and Collaborative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21930)
*Suhyun Han,Kyunghyun Cho,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MindTailor框架，通过分析用户历史发帖构建案例表述，并基于多种咨询策略的协作批评机制生成个性化情感支持回复。在ReddiSupp数据集上的评估表明，该方法在同理心、个性化、理解力和总体偏好上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化情感支持方法主要捕捉用户当前状态（情绪、角色、情境），忽视了塑造当前问题的形成性经历（历史经验）。本文旨在利用用户历史帖子信息来生成更具深度的个性化情感支持回复。

Method: 提出MindTailor框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 从用户发帖历史中构建案例表述（case formulation）；(2) 通过基于多种咨询策略的协作批评机制（collaborative critique）迭代优化回复。同时构建了ReddiSupp数据集（798条Reddit帖子及其历史记录）。

Result: 通过LLM-as-a-Judge评估、专家人工评估和用户研究发现，MindTailor在同理心、个性化、理解力和总体偏好上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 引入用户历史信息能够显著提升情感支持回复的个性化和同理心水平，基于多种咨询策略的协作批评机制是有效的优化手段。

Abstract: As mental health concerns continue to rise globally, social media has emerged as a vital space where individuals seek emotional support. While prior work on personalized emotional support has leveraged seekers' emotional states, personas, and situational context, these approaches primarily capture the seeker's current state, overlooking the formative experiences that shape present concerns. In this work, we propose MindTailor, a framework that generates personalized emotional support responses by constructing a case formulation from the seeker's post history and iteratively refining responses through collaborative critique among counselor agents grounded in distinct counseling strategies. To enable research on this history-aware task, we construct ReddiSupp, a dataset of 798 Reddit posts paired with seekers' prior post histories. Through LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation, expert human evaluation, and a user study with seekers, we demonstrate that MindTailor outperforms baselines across these evaluations, improving empathy, personalization, understanding, and achieving the highest overall preference.

</details>


### [360] [Beyond Value Benchmarks: Measuring Value-Structure Alignment in Large Language Models via Symmetric Q-Sorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21939)
*Jingting Zheng,Yuqi Ren,Linhao Yu,Yongqi Leng,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Q方法论的对称性人机评估框架，通过140项道德陈述的强制分布排序来测量LLM与人类在价值结构上的对齐程度。对12个LLM进行了240次重复Q排序实验，发现模型间存在显著的异质性、对生成随机性的敏感度差异以及局部错位，表明全局分数可能掩盖区域性的价值结构扭曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM道德评估多依赖项目级行为指标，无法捕获模型如何在结构层面上对竞争性价值进行优先级排序，缺乏对价值系统整体对齐性的测量方法。

Method: 基于Q方法论构建对称的人机评估框架，使用140项道德陈述和9列强制分布排序协议；对LLM通过严格排序并确定性映射到Q分类桶中；以35人的人类参考样本建立三因子参考几何结构；对12个LLM在两种温度设置下进行240次复制Q排序，采用Procrustes相似度（φ）和RSA Spearman相关（ρ）量化结构对齐性。

Result: 不同模型家族间存在显著异质性；模型对生成随机性有不同的敏感度；存在局部错位现象，即全局分数良好可能掩盖区域性的价值结构扭曲；基于排序和基于分桶的分析高度一致，但提示措辞引入显著方差。

Conclusion: 评估价值结构对齐性为传统逐项道德基准提供了重要的结构性补充，有助于更全面地理解LLM的道德推理能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in contexts requiring complex moral reasoning and value trade-offs. However, existing evaluations typically rely on item-level behavioral metrics, which fail to capture how models structurally prioritize competing values as a cohesive system. To address this, we propose a symmetric human-LLM evaluation framework, grounded in Q methodology, to measure value-structure alignment. Under our protocol, humans and models sort an identical 140-item moral statement set into a shared nine-column forced distribution; for LLMs, we elicit strict rankings and deterministically map them to Q-sort buckets. Using a human reference sample ($N=35$), we establish a stable three-factor reference geometry specific to this instrument and sample. We evaluate 12 LLMs across four model families via 240 replicated Q-sorts at two temperature settings, quantifying structural alignment via Procrustes similarity ($φ$) and RSA-based Spearman correlation ($ρ$). Our results reveal significant cross-family heterogeneity, model-specific sensitivity to generation stochasticity and localized misalignment, which demonstrate that favorable global scores can obscure underlying regional distortions. While rank- and bucket-based analyses remain highly consistent, prompt phrasing introduces notable variance. Ultimately, assessing value-structure alignment provides a crucial structural complement to traditional itemwise moral benchmarks.

</details>


### [361] [Are Multilingual Models Actually Improving? Isolating True Cross-Lingual Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21954)
*Prasoon Bajpai,Eleftheria Briakou,Colin Cherry,Preethi Jyothi,Vihari Piratla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为Hardness Adjusted Transfer (HAT) Score的新指标，用于更准确地衡量跨语言迁移强度，并通过对20个多语言模型和3个主流基准的分析，揭示了关于模型规模、时间进展与迁移能力关系的三个关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨语言迁移强度的度量方法将迁移改进与源语言准确率的一般性提升混为一谈，缺乏能够可靠衡量真实迁移能力的指标。

Method: 提出Hardness Adjusted Transfer (HAT) Score作为替代指标，在20个多样化语言模型和3个主流多语言基准上进行系统分析。

Result: 1) 小模型的跨语言迁移并未失效；2) 随模型规模增大，跨语言迁移能力的提升速度比预期更慢；3) 随时间推移，跨语言迁移能力取得了明显进展。

Conclusion: HAT Score能够更可靠地捕捉迁移强度，基于该指标的实证分析修正了以往对跨语言迁移能力的若干认知。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer is a model's ability to generalize capabilities from well-represented source languages to under-represented target languages. Existing measures of a model's transfer strength conflate improvements in transfer with general improvements to accuracy in the source language. We advocate for an alternate metric that reliably captures transfer strength called Hardness Adjusted Transfer (HAT) Score, and use it to derive multiple insights on factors influencing transfer strength. Our analysis across twenty diverse language models and three popular mainstream multilingual benchmarks argues that 1) transfer in small models is not broken, 2) we are making slower than expected progress in cross-lingual transfer with model size, and 3) we have made clear progress over time.

</details>


### [362] [OpenBioRQ: Unsolved Biomedical Research Questions for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21959)
*Minbyul Jeong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenBioRQ，一个包含12,553个未解决生物医学研究问题的检索增强型智能体基准。现有基准忽略了引用虚假问题（链接正确但论文不支持主张），而OpenBioRQ通过无答案键的开放问题来测试模型的忠诚度与弃权能力。最难的子集上，最强智能体仅解决约17-60%，且存在智能体崩溃现象（模型在最难问题上停止使用工具）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准存在引用虚假问题——模型生成的链接能解析（99%+可解析率），但约15.9%链接到错误的论文。现有基准因使用有固定答案键的问题而遗漏了这一失败模式：模型可以复现答案键中的来源，而非独立验证来源是否支持主张。

Method: 构建包含12,553个未解决生物医学研究问题的基准，覆盖12个领域。通过三种开源参考模型筛选难度，以真实后续证据而非参数知识验证开放性。使用冻结的逐问题检查表提高评分一致性（Spearman从0.35提升至0.82）。

Result: 开源参考模型仅解决约17%，三个前沿智能体（Gemini-3-Pro、Opus-4.7、GPT-5.5）覆盖29-60%范围。观察到智能体在最难问题上出现崩溃——停止使用工具。对最易崩溃的模型，完全阻止工具使用后得分几乎不变。

Conclusion: 该基准具有难度高、非饱和（最强智能体仍留33-40%未解决）和区分能力强等特点。工具在最需要的地方反而失效，揭示了当前智能体系统在复杂推理场景下的根本性局限。

Abstract: A working citation looks like proof -- but the fact that a link resolves does not mean the cited paper supports the claim. I find that current agentic models rarely fabricate citations (over $99\%$ resolve), yet roughly $15.9\%$ link to the wrong paper. Existing benchmarks miss this failure mode: when a question has a fixed answer key, a model can reproduce the expected source from that key rather than independently verifying that the source supports the claim. I introduce \textbf{\openbiorq{}}, a retrieval-grounded agentic benchmark of $12{,}553$ unsolved biomedical research questions across $12$ domains that treats open questions as a faithfulness-and-abstention probe. To my knowledge, this is the first biomedical benchmark to combine an agentic setting -- where the model must issue multiple tool calls -- with unsolved questions that have no answer key. Openness is verified against real follow-up evidence rather than a model's parametric knowledge. Difficulty is empirical: I anchor it on questions that three open-weight reference models fail to answer, rather than on subjective hardness labels. On this hardest subset, held-out models from the same lineage as the difficulty anchors solve only ~17%, while three independent frontier agents (Gemini-3-Pro, Opus-4.7, GPT-5.5) span a wide 29-60% range. The benchmark is thus hard, non-saturating (the best agent still leaves ~33-40\% unsolved), and discriminating across capability tiers. Beyond difficulty, I observe agentic collapse on the hardest questions, where agents stop using their tools. For the most collapse-prone model, blocking tool access entirely barely changes its score -- so tools stop paying off exactly where they are needed most. A frozen per-question checklist raises inter-judge agreement from Spearman 0.35 to 0.82.

</details>


### [363] [Can LLMs Control Readability? A Multi-Dimensional Evaluation Framework for CEFR-Controlled Arabic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21981)
*Nour Rabih,Chatrine Qwaider,Ted Briscoe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出多维度评估框架，验证指令跟随型LLM在CEFR可控阿拉伯语文本生成中的能力。实验表明，结构化提示（特别是结合词汇约束的CEFR引导提示）显著提升了与参考语言特征的一致性（余弦相似度0.91）和可读性预测一致性（0.99），为自适应教育系统中可读性感知的阿拉伯语文本生成提供了实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能生成流畅阿拉伯语文本，但其能否可靠控制文本可读性级别尚不明确，缺乏系统性评估方法。

Method: 构建多维度评估框架，整合：受控提示策略、基于Taha-19模型的可读性自动预测、词汇约束验证及句法复杂度分析。

Result: 结构化提示显著提升CEFR级别对齐效果，其中CEFR引导结合词汇约束的提示策略达到最高参考语言特征一致性（0.91）和近完美的可读性预测一致性（0.99）；无约束提示则控制能力薄弱。

Conclusion: 为将可读性感知的阿拉伯语文本生成集成到自适应教育系统中奠定了实证基础。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate fluent Arabic text, their ability to reliably control readability levels remains unclear. We propose a multi-dimensional evaluation framework for Common European Framework of Reference for Language (CEFR)-controlled Arabic text generation, assessing whether instruction-following LLMs can serve as reliable generators for adaptive language learning. Our framework integrates controlled prompting, automatic readability prediction using a validated Taha-19 model, lexical constraint validation, and syntactic complexity profiling. Results show that structured prompting substantially improves CEFR alignment. In particular, CEFR-guided prompting with lexical constraints achieves the highest conformity to reference linguistic profiles (0.91 cosine similarity) and near-perfect agreement with predicted readability levels (0.99), while unconstrained prompting exhibits weak control. These findings establish an empirical foundation for integrating readability-aware Arabic text generation into adaptive educational systems.

</details>


### [364] [Adding Robust Code-Switching Capabilities to High Performance Multilingual ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21990)
*Enes Yavuz Ugan,Alexander Waibel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯因子化适配方法，在不损害预训练多语言ASR模型单语能力的前提下，通过少量合成数据高效集成语码转换知识，在CSW词上降低32.87%转录错误，整体WER提升5.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语码转换（CSW）在现实场景中对多语言ASR系统构成挑战，微调合成CSW数据会退化强单语基线性能，需要在不覆盖已有能力的情况下扩展模型处理复杂CSW（含跨语言形态变化）的能力。

Method: 提出贝叶斯因子化适配（Bayesian factorized adaptation），通过学习将切换相关知识与强预训练模型高效集成，仅需少量合成数据，避免覆盖已有参数。

Result: CSW词转录错误降低32.87%，整体WER提升5.31%，且单语性能保持不变。

Conclusion: 有效的CSW适配更依赖于知识集成策略而非数据复杂性，贝叶斯因子化适配能够在不牺牲单语能力的前提下显著提升语码切换场景下的ASR性能。

Abstract: Code-switching (CSW) remains challenging for large multi-lingual ASR systems in real-world deployment. While fine-tuning on synthetic CSW data is possible, it generally degrades strong monolingual baselines. Our goal is to preserve these capabilities while extending models to handle complex code-switching, including morphological variations across languages. We propose Bayesian factorized adaptation, which learns to efficiently integrate switching-relevant knowledge into strong pretrained models without overwriting existing capabilities. Requiring only a small amount of synthetic data, our approach reduces transcription errors by 32.87% on code-switched words while improving overall WER by 5.31%, all while maintaining mono-lingual performance. Our results demonstrate that effective CSW adaptation depends more on knowledge integration than data complexity.

</details>


### [365] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for Grapheme-to-Phoneme Conversion: A Japanese Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22009)
*Tomoki Koriyama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对30多个大语言模型在日语G2P转换任务上进行了基准测试，发现最优模型达到了0.52%的字符错误率，显著优于传统形态分析器（1.03%），其中解析模式（LLM进行形态分析+规则转换）优于直接模式（LLM直接预测读音），且LLM预测的假名用于TTS可获得比端到端TTS更好的发音效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: G2P转换对于可控且鲁棒的文本转语音系统至关重要，大语言模型凭借其广泛的 linguistic knowledge 为此提供了有前景的方案，但缺乏在日语G2P任务上的系统性评估。

Method: 在3000条人工标注句子上对30多个LLM进行基准测试，对比传统的形态分析器。评估两种提示策略：解析模式（LLM进行形态分析后规则转换假名）和直接模式（LLM直接预测假名读音）。

Result: 最优LLM在假名字符错误率上达到0.52%以下，传统最佳工具为1.03%。大多数模型上解析模式优于直接模式。模型规模、版本和日语专项训练是关键因素。

Conclusion: LLM在日语G2P任务上显著超越传统方法；基于规则的后续处理可减轻LLM处理复杂发音规则的负担；LLM预测的假名输入TTS可获得优于端到端TTS的发音效果。

Abstract: Grapheme-to-phoneme (G2P) conversion is essential for controllable and robust text-to-speech, and large language models (LLMs), with broad linguistic knowledge, offer a promising approach. We benchmarked over 30 LLMs on Japanese G2P, comparing them with conventional morphological analyzers on 3000 manually annotated sentences. We evaluated two prompting strategies: a parse mode, where the LLM performs morphological analysis followed by rule-based kana conversion, and a direct mode, where the LLM directly predicts kana readings. The results show that model size, version, and Japanese-specialized training are key factors, with the best LLMs achieving kana character error rate below 0.52\% vs. the best conventional tool (1.03\%). Parse mode outperforms direct mode for most models, as rule-based post-processing relieves the LLM of handling complex pronunciation rules. We also show that feeding LLM-predicted kana into a kana-input TTS yields better pronunciation than end-to-end TTS.

</details>


### [366] [NL2Scratch: An Executable Benchmark and Evaluation for Block-Based Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22061)
*Heejin Do,Alexandre Ballenghien,Yang Wu,April Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 NL2Scratch，一个面向 Scratch 编程语言的自然语言到代码生成基准，包含 311,648 个解析器验证的 NL-程序对，并设计了语义对齐一致性（SAC）指标来评估描述与程序之间的语义匹配，实验表明词法相似度与语义对齐之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NL2Code 研究主要针对文本编程语言，而 Scratch 作为基于块的编程环境具有事件驱动、视觉组合、并发脚本等特性，传统评估方法不适用，缺乏专门的自然语言到 Scratch 的生成基准。

Method: 从真实 Scratch 项目中提取程序并配对语义对齐的自然语言描述，构建 311,648 个解析器验证的 NL-程序对；提出可解释的槽级指标 SAC（语义对齐一致性）用于测量描述与程序间的语义一致性；基于 SAC 构建了语义验证的 23,594 示例池和 800 个槽平衡的诊断基准。

Result: 实验发现词法相似度（token-level F1 > 0.93）与语义对齐（SAC）之间存在显著差距，模型在较长示例上难以达到完美 SAC；错误集中在动作、条件和数值参数等操作槽上，传统指标无法揭示这些失败模式。

Conclusion: 现有 NL2Code 评估指标不足，需要语义级别的评估方法；NL2Scratch 基准和 SAC 指标为面向块的编程语言的 NL2Code 研究提供了更可靠的评估基础。

Abstract: Block-based programming environments such as Scratch are widely used in early programming education, yet natural-language-to-code (NL2Code) research has focused primarily on text-based languages. Scratch programs are event-driven, visually compositional, and distributed across concurrent scripts, making conventional NL2Code assumptions and evaluation insufficient. We introduce NL2Scratch, an executable benchmark for natural-language-to-Scratch generation comprising 311,648 parser-valid NL--program pairs, whose program side is extracted from real Scratch projects and paired with semantically aligned NL descriptions. For reliable evaluation beyond surface overlap, we propose Semantic Alignment Consistency (SAC), an interpretable slot-level metric for measuring semantic agreement between descriptions and programs. With SAC, we construct a semantically validated pool of 23,594 examples, and a slot-balanced 800 diagnostic benchmark. Experiments across instruction-tuned and fine-tuned LLMs reveal a notable gap between lexical similarity and semantic alignment: models achieving token-level F1 above 0.93 often fail to attain perfect SAC, particularly on longer examples. Errors concentrate on operational slots like actions, conditions, and numeric arguments, exposing failure modes largely invisible under conventional metrics.

</details>


### [367] [Where Does the Signal Live? A Web Data Recipe for Medical Encoder Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22079)
*Bofeng Huang,Jacques Sun,Diane Bouchacourt,Nicolas Barascud,Fajwel Fogel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补策略（医学术语密度过滤与信号增强改写）来筛选和优化网络数据，用于编码器MLM预训练，并在法语医学领域构建了FineMed语料库和DoctoBERT模型，在公开基准和临床NER任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学等密集术语领域的编码器通常依赖小规模、人工筛选的语料库进行预训练，这限制了可扩展性和写作风格多样性，尤其在非英语临床场景中问题更为突出。网络级数据 curation 是否能同样惠及编码器MLM尚不清楚。

Method: 提出两个互补机制：(1) 医学术语密度过滤——挑选富含医学术语的文档；(2) 信号增强改写——使用LLM将文档改写为术语更密集、实体上下文更丰富的变体。在法语医学NLP上实例化该方案。

Result: 医学术语密度过滤在医学下游任务上优于广泛使用的教育质量过滤，且二者互补；信号增强改写单独使用即可提升原始网络数据表现，与过滤后的网络数据混合使用效果最佳。

Conclusion: 该方案构建了FineMed法语医学预训练语料库和DoctoBERT系列模型，在DrBenchmark公开基准和一项专有临床NER任务上均达到法语医学编码器的SOTA水平。

Abstract: Web data curation has been widely studied for decoder Large Language Model (LLM) pretraining. Encoders for dense-terminology domains such as medicine, by contrast, are pretrained on small, manually-curated corpora that limit scalability and writing style diversity, a bottleneck even more severe in non-English clinical settings. Whether web-scale data curation also benefits encoder Masked Language Modeling (MLM) in a dense-terminology domain remains an open question. To address this, we introduce two complementary levers. Medical-term density filtering selects documents rich in medical terms. Signal-amplifying rephrasing uses an LLM to rewrite documents into denser variants with broader entity contexts. We instantiate the recipe on French medical NLP. The medical-term density filter outperforms the widely-used educational quality filter on downstream medical tasks, and the two complement each other. Signal-amplifying rephrasing alone improves on raw web data, and mixing it with filtered web data produces the largest gain. The recipe yields FineMed, a French medical pretraining corpus, and DoctoBERT, a state-of-the-art French medical encoder family evaluated on both the public benchmark DrBenchmark and a proprietary clinical Named Entity Recognition (NER) task.

</details>


### [368] [Plurification in/of language technology -- The integration of culture in next-generation AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22097)
*Gertraud Koch,Fausto Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何在自然语言处理（NLP）中操作化"文化"概念，并提出文化对齐不能仅通过增加"其他文化"的示例来实现，而需要多元认识论——允许多种本地化的认知方式。通过使用语言技术设计的五层技术活动模型（社会技术模型），本文系统化整理了NLP中处理文化的方法。分析表明，尽管NLP研究在文化敏感性系统方面取得进展，但多数方法仍停留在输出或表征层面，未能解决权力、治理和社会语境等深层问题。结论指出，操作化文化需要超越技术适应，采取反思性和多元的社会技术路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP系统在文化表征上存在单一化问题，但学界对如何系统性地将文化纳入NLP设计缺乏共识。本文旨在解决"文化"在NLP中如何被操作化这一核心问题，并揭示技术设计中考虑多元文化背景的可能性和局限性。

Method: 采用社会技术视角，利用语言技术设计的五层技术活动模型（技术活动层次模型）作为分析框架，系统收集和整理NLP中处理文化问题的各类方法，并对其进行分析和分类。

Result: NLP研究在文化敏感系统方面取得了一定进展，但多数方法仍停留在输出层或表征层处理"文化"问题，未能触及权力结构、治理机制和社会语境等更深层次的问题。现有方法整体上是局部和不完整的。

Conclusion: 操作化文化不能仅靠技术层面的适配，需要一种反思性的、多元的社会技术方法，在计算形式化的潜力与局限之间进行权衡，以应对多种语言和社会文化背景的多样性。

Abstract: The paper explores how "culture" can be operationalised in Natural Language Processing (NLP) and what this reveals about the possibilities and limits of considering a plurality of cultural backgrounds in technological design. It proposes that cultural alignment cannot be achieved only by adding more examples of "other cultures", rather it requires plural epistemologies: allowing multiple, locally grounded ways of knowing. To analyze how this plurality of knowing can be addressed in NLP, the paper uses a socio-technical model of language technology (LT) design, the five layers of technological activity model, for collecting and systematizing approaches to culture in NLP. The analysis shows that while NLP research has made progress toward more culturally sensitive systems, many approaches remain partial, addressing "culture" primarily at the level of output or representation while leaving deeper questions of power, governance, and social context unresolved. The paper concludes that operationalising culture requires much more than technical adaptation; it suggests a reflexive and plural socio-technical approach that navigates potentials and limits of computational formalisation for accounting multiple linguistic and socio-cultural backgrounds.

</details>


### [369] [From Recognition to Understanding: Unlocking Cognitive Time Series Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22126)
*Xin Qiu,Junlong Tong,Yao Zhang,Yunpu Ma,Wei Zhang,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出时间序列分析与LLM结合效果有限的原因在于任务设置与LLM能力不匹配，为此提出多模态推理基准TSCognition（含约41K QA样本和五项认知任务）及统一框架TSAlign（通过补丁级编码、门控残差注入和多变量融合实现时间序列与LLM语义对齐），实验表明TSAlign在多个基准上超越现有方法且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列分析任务大多被简化为曲线拟合和低层级预测，忽视了真实世界时序决策中的语义、上下文和推理密集型特性，导致LLM的优势未能充分发挥。

Method: 构建TSCognition多模态基准，包含来自15个公开源的现实时序数据和文本信息，围绕五项认知推理任务（解码、 grounding、推断、外推、行动）生成约41K QA样本。提出TSAlign框架，将时间序列编码为紧凑的补丁级表示，通过门控残差注入和多变量融合与LLM嵌入空间中的语义方向对齐。

Result: TSAlign在TSCognition和TimerBed基准上优于现有LLM、VLM和时间序列QA基线方法，同时显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 通过重新设计任务范式和对齐机制，可以更有效地将时间序列分析与LLM的推理能力结合，突破现有性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Time series analysis has recently been coupled with Large Language Models (LLMs) to leverage their reasoning and world knowledge capabilities, yet gains remain limited. We attribute this to a fundamental mismatch between existing task formulations and LLM strengths: most settings reduce time series understanding to curve-fitting systems, focusing on low-level prediction while ignoring the semantic, contextual, and reasoning-intensive nature of real-world temporal decision-making.To address these limitations, we introduce TSCognition, a multimodal benchmark for multi-dimensional time series reasoning. It collects real-world time series and textual information from 15 public sources and constructs approximately 41K QA samples around five cognitive reasoning tasks: Decoding, Grounding, Inferring, Extrapolating, and Acting. Building on this, we further propose TSAlign, a unified framework that encodes time series into compact patch-level representations and aligns them with semantic directions in the LLM embedding space via gated residual injection and multivariate fusion.Experiments show that TSAlign outperforms existing LLM, VLM, and time series QA baselines on TSCognition and the publicly available TimerBed benchmark while substantially reducing computational cost.Code is available at: [https://github.com/EIT-NLP/CognitiveTSR](https://github.com/EIT-NLP/CognitiveTSR)

</details>


### [370] [BioMatrix: Towards a Comprehensive Biological Foundation Model Spanning the Modality Matrix of Sequences, Structures, and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22138)
*Qizhi Pei,Zhimeng Zhou,Yi Duan,Yiyang Zhao,Wei Li,Han Guo,Liang He,Chengping Li,Chang-Yu Hsieh,Conghui He,Rui Yan,Lijun Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BioMatrix 是首个在单一 decoder-only 架构中原生整合分子序列、分子结构、蛋白质序列、蛋白质结构和自然语言的多模态基础模型，通过统一的分词方案将所有模态映射到共享离散 token 空间，在单一 next-token prediction 目标下统一生成，无需外部编码器或适配器，在 80 项生物任务中 77 项达到 SOTA 或竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物基础模型未能同时实现原生多模态与广泛实体覆盖：融合多模态的模型局限于单一实体类型，而覆盖多种实体类型的模型要么缺失结构建模，要么依赖适配器设计导致无法原生生成某些模态。

Method: 基于 Qwen3 语言模型（1.7B 和 4B），通过统一分词方案将分子序列（SMILES/SELFIES）、分子结构、蛋白质序列、蛋白质结构、自然语言映射到共享离散 token 空间；采用单一 next-token prediction 目标，无需外部编码器、投影适配器或模态特定输出头；在 3044 亿 token 上进行持续预训练，涵盖通用/领域文本、分子与蛋白质序列/结构视图、跨模态语料（交织生物分子与科学文本、分子-蛋白质和蛋白质-蛋白质交互数据）。
