To address this gap, we introduce IndicGuard, a multilingual safety guard model and dataset for Indic languages. We construct a high-volume, culturally nuanced safety dataset encompassing ten major Indic languages, systematically curated to capture regional harms, sensitive socio-political contexts, and adversarial jailbreaks. Leveraging this corpus, we fine-tune a 4B-parameter instruction-tuned model based on Gemma-3-4B-IT to serve as a multilingual safety guardrail for real-time content moderation and policy compliance checking. Our empirical evaluations demonstrate that IndicGuard significantly enhances LLM robustness against localized vulnerabilities, achieving high moderation consistency across different conversational turns. Crucially, IndicGuard consistently outperforms the existing baseline model, CultureGuard, across evaluated languages. Finally, we demonstrate that our model effectively generalizes to low-resource Indic languages excluded from training, substantiating the structural robustness and cross-lingual transfer capabilities of the framework.

</details>


### [406] [DynamicMem: A Long-Horizon Memory Benchmark in Real-World Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22877)
*Wenya Xie,Shengming Zhou,Zelin Li,Pouya Parsa,Shuang Zhou,Xinheng Ding,Chinmay Arvind,Guanchu Wang,Vladimir Braverman,Ali Payani,Yantao Zheng,Zirui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DynamicMem是一个合成基准，通过构建15个月、跨16个应用的用户活动轨迹（平均每条220万token、1772个事件）来评估LLM代理的长程记忆能力。基准测试揭示了三个关键问题：记忆准确性随历史长度下降而服务任务准确率不变、系统无法平衡事实保持与更新、93%以上的失败源于记忆检索而非推理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通过短时简化交互评估记忆能力，忽略了真实用户画像的三个核心特性：属性、习惯与偏好的异质性及其在不同时间线上的演化；外部情境（如季节、生活事件）驱动的变化；以及证据稀疏分散在不同应用中而非明确陈述。真实用户隐私限制使得获取长期跨应用数据极为困难。

Method: 构建合成基准DynamicMem，生成15个月的用户活动轨迹，涵盖16个应用；每个用户轨迹平均220万token和1772个grounded事件；画像包含属性、习惯、偏好三类信息且随时间演化，需从跨应用稀疏信号中推断；设置五个季度检查点评估系统随历史增长的扩展能力。

Result: 评测五个代表性系统发现：(1)画像重建准确率随历史长度下降，而服务任务准确率保持不变；(2)没有系统能同时做到保持正确事实和替换变化事实，错误集中在偏好和确切指代命名上；(3)超过93%的失败源于记忆检索环节，而非生成答案的模型。

Conclusion: 最大改进空间在于记忆系统本身而非推理模型；需要设计能更好平衡事实保持与更新的记忆机制，特别关注偏好类信息的追踪和精确指代。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act as personal assistants that must remember a user's profile over months: who they are (attributes), what they routinely do (habits), and what they prefer (preferences), and keep it updated as jobs, routines, and tastes drift. Existing benchmarks evaluate this "memory" ability through short, simplified interactions, missing three core properties of real behavior: the profile is heterogeneous, with attributes, habits, and preferences evolving on different timelines; changes are driven by external context such as seasons and life events; and evidence is rarely stated explicitly, instead scattered across many small actions in different apps that a memory system must infer from. We introduce DynamicMem, a synthetic benchmark that constructs 15 months of activity per user, providing long-term multi-app data that real users' privacy keeps out of reach. It provides user-consistent trajectories averaging 2.2M tokens and 1,772 grounded events per user across 16 applications such as e-commerce, fitness, and social platforms. The profile evolves over this period and is never given explicitly: each attribute, habit, or preference must be inferred from small signals scattered across apps. We evaluate at five quarterly checkpoints to track how systems scale as history grows. Benchmarking five representative systems exposes problems a single accuracy score hides: (i) profile reconstruction degrades with history length while service-task accuracy stays flat, despite both drawing on the same memory; (ii) no system both keeps facts that stay true and replaces facts that change, with errors clustering on preferences and on naming the exact referent; and (iii) over 93% of failures trace to what the memory retrieves, not to the model writing the answer, so the largest room for improvement lies in memory itself. Code: https://wenyaxie023.github.io/DynamicMem/

</details>


### [407] [Explanation-Guided Medical Named Entity Recognition with Stability and Boundary Awareness for Atopic Dermatitis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22886)
*Xueguang Li,Di Lin,Xue Jiang,Yanxi Li,Yugang Chi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种稳定性和边界感知的解释引导式命名实体识别框架，通过扰动分析评估解释稳定性与实体边界敏感性，并采用自适应融合策略结合局部与全局解释，将可靠的解释信号以稳定性、边界感知和一致性约束方式融入模型训练。在中文特应性皮炎临床文本NER数据集上，该方法提升了解释鲁棒性，并在多个NER模型上取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升中文特应性皮炎临床文本中医学命名实体识别的可靠性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出稳定性和边界感知的解释引导NER框架，包括：(1) 基于扰动的解释稳定性与实体边界敏感性分析；(2) 自适应融合策略动态结合局部与全局解释生成更可靠的token级解释；(3) 将融合后的解释信号通过稳定性约束、边界感知约束和一致性约束融入模型训练。

Result: 在中文AD NER数据集上，所提框架提升了解释鲁棒性，在多个NER模型上取得一致性能提升；自适应融合策略相比单一解释方法提供了更稳定的解释和更强的边界感知能力。

Conclusion: 该方法有效将可靠的解释信号融入医学NER训练，同时提升了识别性能和解释可靠性，为可解释医学NER提供了实用且泛化的解决方案，为下游临床决策和医学知识应用提供可靠支持。

Abstract: Objective: This study aims to improve the reliability and robustness of medical named entity recognition (NER) in Chinese atopic dermatitis (AD) clinical texts through explanation-guided learning. Methods: We propose a stability and boundary-aware explanation-guided NER framework. Perturbation-based analysis is used to evaluate explanation stability and entity boundary sensitivity. An adaptive fusion strategy dynamically combines local and global explanation to generate more reliable token-level explanations. The fused explanation signals are further incorporated into model training through stability, boundary-aware, and consistency constraints. Results: Experiments on Chinese AD NER datasets show that the proposed framework improves explanation robustness and achieves consistent performance gains across multiple NER models. The adaptive fusion strategy also provides more stable explanations and stronger boundary perception than individual explanation methods. Conclusion: The proposed method effectively integrates reliable explanation signals into medical NER training, improving both recognition performance and explanation reliability. The framework provides a practical and generalizable solution for explainable medical NER and offers reliable support for downstream clinical decision-making and medical knowledge applications.

</details>


### [408] [Understanding Knowledge Distillation in Post-Training: When It Helps and When It Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22942)
*Xin Liu,Simin Ma,Shujian Liu,Song Wang,Sathish Reddy Indurthi,Haoyun Deng,Lu Wang,Kaiqiang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 系统研究了知识蒸馏在后训练阶段的效果，发现在低数据场景下优于监督微调，但随着数据增多优势减弱；从更强的指令调优教师模型蒸馏即使在数据充足时也能带来显著提升；提出两阶段知识蒸馏策略在低资源场景下持续改进学生模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型计算成本高，在资源受限环境中部署困难；知识蒸馏虽被提出作为解决方案，但以往研究主要关注任务特定或小规模场景，对后训练阶段构建通用指令遵循模型的系统性研究不足。

Method: 使用大规模Tulu 3数据集，系统性对比知识蒸馏与监督微调在不同数据规模下的效果；研究教师模型能力对蒸馏效果的影响；针对领域特定低资源场景，提出两阶段知识蒸馏策略：先使用教师生成的合成标签数据训练，再在人工标注数据上精调。

Result: 知识蒸馏在低数据场景下优于监督微调，但优势随数据增加而减弱；从更强的指令调优教师模型蒸馏在数据充足时仍能带来显著收益；两阶段策略在低资源场景下持续提升学生模型性能。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏在数据稀缺环境下是构建紧凑模型的有效方法；教师模型提供学生难以从训练数据中获取的知识是蒸馏有效的关键；提出的两阶段策略为低资源场景提供了实用的蒸馏指南。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across many tasks, but their high computational cost limits deployment in resource-constrained environments. Knowledge Distillation (KD) offers a practical solution by transferring knowledge from a teacher model of a larger size to a smaller student model. While prior work has mainly examined task-specific or small-scale settings, the post-training stage for building general instruction-following models has received limited attention. In this paper, we conduct a systematic study of KD in post-training using the large-scale Tulu 3 dataset. We find that KD outperforms supervised fine-tuning (SFT) in low-data regimes, but its advantage diminishes as more training data is added. Distilling from a stronger instruction-tuned teacher restores substantial gains even with abundant data, indicating that KD remains effective when the teacher provides knowledge that the student cannot easily acquire from the training data alone. We further study domain-specific, low-resource scenarios and propose a two-stage KD strategy that leverages synthetic teacher-labeled data followed by refinement on human annotations. This method consistently improves student performance, providing practical guidance for building compact models in data-scarce environments.

</details>


### [409] [StatABench: Dataset and Framework for Evaluating Statistical Analysis Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22977)
*Youxin Zhu,Yixuan Ding,Peng Lai,Longyue Wang,Bingyi Jing,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出StatABench基准，系统评估LLM在统计分析任务上的能力，包含404道封闭题和30道开放建模题。实验显示最强模型GPT-5.1仅达68.6%准确率，揭示LLM在工具推理、方法决策和端到端建模方面仍存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在统计分析领域的评估基准在范围和格式上存在局限，缺乏系统全面的评估体系。

Method: 构建StatABench基准，包含Stat-Closed（404题，18个统计主题，多种题型）和Stat-Open（30道开放式建模任务）；采用LangChain MCP框架和数据科学智能体进行评估；使用LLM-as-Judge协议评估开放题解答。

Result: GPT-5.1在Stat-Closed上仅68.6%，最佳开源模型60.6%；Stat-Open上最优智能体框架平均得分为61.86。

Conclusion: 当前LLM距离可靠的统计分析能力仍有差距，在工具化推理、方法学决策和端到端统计建模方面面临持续挑战。

Abstract: Statistical analysis is a broad, complex field requiring both domain knowledge and tool proficiency. While prior work has evaluated large language models (LLMs) in this domain, existing benchmarks remain limited in scope and format. To bridge this gap, we introduce StatABench (Statistical AnalysisBenchmark), a benchmark designed to systematically assess LLMs' statistical analysis capabilities. StatABench comprises two complementary components: Stat-Closed, containing 404 questions across 18 statistical topics in multiple formats (multiple-choice, fill-in-the-blank, decision-making, and practical application), and Stat-Open, featuring 30 complex open-ended modeling tasks adapted from professional competitions. We evaluate diverse LLMs using the LangChain MCP framework and multiple data science agents, and assess Stat-Open solutions via a validated LLM-as-Judge protocol. Experiments show that even GPT-5.1 achieves only 68.6% on Stat-Closed, while the best open-source model reaches 60.6%. On Stat-Open, the top agent framework scores 61.86 on average. These results reveal the gap between current LLMs and reliable statistical analysis, highlighting persistent challenges in tool-grounded reasoning, methodological decision-making, and end-to-end statistical modeling.

</details>


### [410] [Predicate Importance Estimation and Decoupled Rationale-Score Distillation for Entity Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22992)
*Keunha Kim,Yoonjin Jang,Hyeon-gu Lee,Sihyung Kim,Youngjoong Ko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PIE（谓词重要性估计）和 DRSD（解耦理由-分数蒸馏）两个互补模块，分别通过谓词感知的实体嵌入和教师 LLM 到小模型的解耦蒸馏，提升知识图谱实体对齐在谓词名称变化和不完整邻域场景下的性能，并利用置信度与理由的差异实现人机协同验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级 KG-RAG 系统需融合来自异构数据库的公共和领域特定知识图谱，这依赖于实体对齐（EA）。然而在谓词名称变化和局部邻域不完整的情况下，纯词汇匹配不足，现有 EA 方法面临挑战。

Method: 1) PIE：去除1跳三元组的主语信息，编码得到的无主三元组，并通过可学习的谓词重要性权重聚合，构建谓词感知的实体嵌入。2) DRSD：教师LLM通过不同提示生成伪答案，训练蒸馏后的小语言模型（SLM）；将二分类EA标签转为文本监督，将置信度分数估计与标签一致的理由解耦，使SLM学习任务特定推理且保留较少标签偏差的置信信号。

Result: 实验表明 PIE 和 DRSD 能够提升 EA 分类性能。同时，由于 DRSD 将置信度估计与决策解耦，两者不一致时可标记为不确定预测以供人工审查，实现自动接受与人在回路验证之间的实用差异。

Conclusion: PIE 和 DRSD 有效解决了异构知识图谱实体对齐中谓词名称变化和不完整邻域的问题，并通过解耦设计支持人机协同的可靠EA流程，适用于工业级 KG-RAG 系统。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) are increasingly used as structured context for Large Language Models (LLMs), but industrial KG-RAG systems often need to integrate public and domain-specific KGs constructed from heterogeneous databases. This integration relies on Entity Alignment (EA), where lexical matching alone is insufficient under predicate-name variation and incomplete local neighborhoods. We address EA for KG integration by constructing a pairwise EA dataset and proposing two complementary modules: Predicate Importance Estimation (PIE) and Decoupled Rationale-Score Distillation (DRSD). PIE is a compact embedding-based approach that removes the subject information from each 1-hop triple, encodes the resulting subjectless triples, and aggregates them with learnable predicate-importance weights to build predicate-aware entity embeddings. DRSD trains a distilled small language model (SLM) with pseudo-answers produced by a teacher LLM through distinct prompts. By converting binary EA labels into text-based supervision and decoupling confidence-score estimation from label-consistent rationales, DRSD enables the SLM to learn task-specific reasoning while retaining a less label-biased confidence signal. Experiments show that PIE and DRSD improve EA classification. Moreover, because DRSD decouples confidence-score estimation from the decision, a discrepancy between the two flags an uncertain prediction for human review, thereby enabling a practical discrepancy between automatic acceptance and human-in-the-loop verification.

</details>


### [411] [Machine Translation and Post-Editing: Comparative Evaluation of Different MT Systems and Post-Editor Groups in Specialised Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23002)
*Joachim Minder,Alexandra Mestivier,Natalie Kübler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了英译法专业翻译中三种机器翻译系统（DeepL、eTranslation、Systran）的翻译质量，以及两组译者（语言学家/翻译人员与NLP专家）的译后编辑效果，发现系统间和编辑组间在术语准确性和流畅度上存在显著差异，凸显了领域知识在专业翻译中的重要性及机器翻译在特定用途语言中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估专业翻译场景下机器翻译和译后编辑的质量，比较不同MT系统的表现以及不同背景的译后编辑者（语言专家与NLP专家）的编辑效果。

Method: 采用三种MT系统（DeepL、eTranslation、Systran）进行英译法翻译，邀请两组译后编辑者（语言学家/翻译人员与NLP专家）进行译后编辑，基于适用于MT和PE评估的错误类型学进行错误标注和质量评估。

Result: 三种MT系统之间以及两组译后编辑者之间均存在显著差异，尤其在术语准确性和流畅度方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 研究强调了领域知识在专业翻译中的关键作用，同时揭示了MT系统在特定用途语言（LSP）翻译中的性能不稳定性和局限性。

Abstract: This article aims to evaluate the quality of machine translation (MT) and post-editing (PE) in the context of specialised translation from English into French. Three MT systems (DeepL, eTranslation and Systran) were compared, and two groups of post-editors -linguists/translators and NLP experts -were asked to perform post-editing. Translation assessment is based on error annotation using an error typology adapted to MT and PE evaluation. The results reveal significant differences between the three MT systems and the two groups of post-editors, particularly in terms of terminological accuracy and fluency. This study highlights the importance of domain knowledge in specialised translation, as well as the limitations and variable performance of MT systems in language for specific purposes (LSP).

</details>


### [412] [Have You Ever Seen Them? Entity-level Membership Inference through Interrogating Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23030)
*Yiran Zhu,Ziqi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出实体级成员推理攻击，在仅标签的黑盒设置下判断LLM是否在训练中使用了与目标实体相关的信息。通过五种基于线索的询问策略，在人物实体数据集上AUC达0.97，平衡准确率提升6.0%-17.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推理研究多聚焦于样本级（判断特定样本是否用于训练），但LLM表现出类似人类记忆的行为：虽不一定逐字记忆样本，却能累积并泄露关于现实世界实体的知识。这启发作者探究能否像采访人类一样询问LLM，以揭示其对实体信息的暴露情况。

Method: 形式化定义了实体级成员推理任务，在仅标签（label-only）黑盒设置下，考虑线索约束、输入约束和模型约束。基于此形式化建立了任务可行的充要条件，并实例化五种询问策略：利用有限实体线索构建提示，引发实体相关响应，从生成文本的语义特征中推断成员关系。构建了实体级数据集，并将SOTA样本级标签方法适配为基线。

Result: 在人物实体上的实验表明，所提方法AUC最高达0.97，平衡准确率比最佳适配基线提升6.0%-17.5%。

Conclusion: 实体级成员推理是可行的且比样本级方法更有效，揭示了LLM在实体层面的隐私泄露风险，为评估LLM隐私合规性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) raise growing concerns about privacy leakage and copyright compliance. Membership inference is a key tool for assessing such risks, but existing studies mainly focus on whether specific samples or sample-based data units are used for training. We argue that LLMs exhibit a human-memory-like behavior: an LLM may not memorize a specific sample verbatim, yet it can accumulate and reveal knowledge about a real-world entity from scattered mentions. This analogy motivates us to examine whether an LLM can be interrogated like a human interviewee to reveal its exposure to entity-related information. Motivated by this question, we propose entity-level membership inference, which determines whether information related to a target entity is used in LLM training. We study this task in the practical label-only black-box setting, where only generated texts are observable. We formalize the task under clue, input, and model constraints, establish the necessary and sufficient conditions for its feasibility, and instantiate five interrogation strategies based on this formalization. The strategies use limited entity clues to construct prompts, elicit entity-related responses, and infer membership from semantic features among the generated texts. We construct entity-level datasets and adapt state-of-the-art sample-level label-only methods to the entity-level setting as baselines. Experiments on person entities show that our methods achieve AUC up to 0.97 and bring gains of 6.0%--17.5% in Balanced Accuracy over the best adapted baseline.

</details>


### [413] [Training Open Models for Agentic Phone Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23049)
*Zhengyang Tang,Xin Lai,Pengyuan Lyu,Xinyuan Wang,Tianyi Bai,Chenxin Li,Yiduo Guo,Huawen Shen,Yuxuan Liu,Junyi Li,Zhengyao Fang,Yang Ding,Yi Zhang,Weinong Wang,Xingran Zhou,Liang Wu,Fei Tang,Sunqi Fan,Shangpin Peng,Zheng Ruan,Anran Zhang,Benyou Wang,Ji-Rong Wen,Rui Yan,Chengquan Zhang,Han Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhoneBuddy提出了一种结合真实应用环境和模拟应用环境（PhoneWorld）的训练方案，用于训练通用手机操作智能体。通过混合强化学习（混合RL）训练，模型在真实手机150个任务上的成功率达到45.33%，在AndroidWorld上达到83.2%，表明模拟环境训练并非真实环境RL的替代品，而是一种可扩展、可重置、可自动检查的互补来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实手机环境（运行真实应用）在部署时至关重要，但存在速度慢、有状态性、副作用、难以重置和验证等问题；而可扩展的模拟环境只能近似真实行为，两者之间存在差距，训练可靠的开源手机操作智能体仍具挑战。

Method: PhoneBuddy首先构建共享监督微调阶段，从真实环境和模拟环境（PhoneWorld，从真实GUI使用结构重建可运行的模拟应用）中收集轨迹；然后比较纯真实环境RL和混合环境（真实+模拟）RL的效果。

Result: 在真实手机的150任务人工评估中，监督微调后成功率36.67%，真实环境RL后40.67%，混合RL后45.33%。在AndroidWorld上，对应进展为60.3%→77.2%→83.2%。混合RL在应用内和小型应用任务上增益最强，跨应用长流程任务仍是开放挑战。

Conclusion: 模拟环境训练不是真实环境RL的替代品，而是可扩展、可重置、可自动检查交互的互补来源。模拟环境可提供大量低成本训练数据，但真实环境的反馈仍是提升性能的关键。

Abstract: Phones are becoming an important execution surface for general-purpose agents, but training open models for reliable phone use remains difficult because the environment that matters at deployment, real devices running real apps, is slow, stateful, side-effectful, and hard to reset or verify, while scalable mock environments only approximate real behavior. We present PhoneBuddy, a training recipe and open-model line for agentic phone use that combines a real-app environment with a mock-app environment, PhoneWorld, which reconstructs runnable mock apps from real GUI usage structure. PhoneBuddy first builds a shared supervised fine-tuning stage from trajectories collected in both environments, then compares real-app RL against mixed RL across both environments. Across a 150-task human evaluation on real phones spanning apps, mini-apps, and cross-app workflows, task success rate improves from 36.67\% after supervised fine-tuning to 40.67\% after real-app RL and 45.33\% after mixed RL. On AndroidWorld, the same progression rises from 60.3\% to 77.2\% to 83.2\%. These results show that mock-app training is not a replacement for real-app RL, but a complementary source of scalable, resettable, and automatically checked interaction. The gains are strongest on app and mini-app tasks, while long-horizontal cross-app workflows remain an important open challenge.

</details>


### [414] [PIVOTSBench: Evaluating Fine-Grained Interpersonal Relationship Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23092)
*Shuxiang Zhang,Yiting Yin,Wenxuan Song,Yuhang Wu,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PIVOTS是一个评估多模态大模型理解人际关系能力的基准，基于Social-IQ 2.0和YouTube数据构建，包含双向人际关系维度预测及相关视觉线索识别任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类天生具备理解细粒度人际关系的能力，但现有的多模态大模型在这一领域尚未被充分探索，缺乏系统的评估基准。

Method: 从Social-IQ 2.0和YouTube数据构建PIVOTS基准，设计双向人际关系维度预测任务及辅助的视觉关键线索识别任务，对开源和商业多模态大模型进行评测与消融研究。

Result: 评估了多种专有和开源MLLM，通过消融研究分析了视觉模态和对话中显式社会角色信息的影响，以及联合预测和成对预测设置对评分效果的提升。

Conclusion: PIVOTS填补了MLLM在细粒度人际关系理解评估方面的空白，揭示了当前模型在该任务上的表现与局限性，为未来研究提供了基准与方向。

Abstract: Humans possess an innate ability to understand fine-grained interpersonal relationships, which is central to everyday social interactions. Although such reasoning is inherently multimodal, it remains largely unexplored by existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs). To address this gap, we introduce PIVOTS, the first benchmark built from Social-IQ 2.0 and YouTube data to evaluate MLLMs' ability to predict bidirectional interpersonal relationship dimensions grounded in established psychology research. In addition, PIVOTS includes auxiliary tasks that assess models' ability to identify and leverage the critical visual cues underlying such predictions. We evaluate both proprietary and open-source MLLMs and conduct detailed ablation studies to analyze the effects of visual modalities and explicit social role information in conversational utterances. We further examine how joint and pairwise prediction settings benefit MLLMs in scoring bidirectional PIVOTS dimensions. Project page and resources: https://flynnzhangsx.github.io/PIVOTSBench/ .

</details>


### [415] [A Dual-Track Framework for Template-Constrained LaTeX Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23107)
*Chung Cheuk Hei,Liu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个双轨框架（Dual-Track Framework），将模板格式化与文档处理解耦，通过离线轨道提取模板约束、在线轨道混合执行（LLM处理推理密集型任务+规则引擎处理确定性任务），在7个LaTeX模板和56篇论文上实现了更好的结构保真度和编译成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（规则式转换器或纯端到端LLM生成）在处理结构化Markdown到LaTeX模板转换时存在局限：前者无法正确处理资源插入和模板特定约束，后者易产生语义漂移和难以调试的幻觉。

Method: 提出双轨框架：1) 离线轨道：将模板约束提取为可复用的清单；2) 在线轨道：混合执行管线，将LLM限制在推理密集型组件（语义元数据、参考文献、复杂图表布局），而规则引擎处理确定性任务。

Result: 在7个LaTeX模板和56篇已发表研究论文上的实验表明，该方法相比基线方法具有更好的结构保真度、满足多样化的布局约束，并实现了更高的编译成功率。

Conclusion: 双轨框架通过LLM与规则引擎的混合执行方式，有效解决了Markdown到LaTeX模板转换中的结构完整性和语义漂移问题。

Abstract: With the increasing demands for advanced document conversion, mapping structured Markdown drafts into template-compliant formats like LaTeX remains a challenge. Existing approaches largely depend on either deterministic rule-based converters or pure end-to-end Large Language Model (LLM) generation. The former fails to correctly handle asset insertions and template-specific constraints, while the latter tends to induce semantic drift, leading to hallucinations that are difficult to debug. To address these limitations, we introduce a robust Dual-Track Framework that systematically decouples template formatting from document processing: an offline track extracts template constraints into a reusable manifest, while an online track implements a hybrid execution pipeline. This pipeline confines LLM usage exclusively to reasoning-intensive components (e.g., semantic metadata, bibliographic references, and complex visual/tabular layouts) while delegating rule-based engines for deterministic processing. Empirical evaluation across 7 LaTeX templates and 56 published research papers demonstrates that our method preserves better structural fidelity, satisfies diverse layout constraints, and achieves a higher compilation success rate compared to the previous baselines.

</details>


### [416] [PRIDE: Privileged Information-enhanced Distillation for Empathetic Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23124)
*Jiaqiang Wu,Zhouan Zhu,Shangfei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PRIDE方法，利用特权信息增强知识蒸馏，在保持共情对话生成质量的同时实现模型压缩，在资源受限环境下仍能获得与教师模型相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在共情对话生成中表现优异，但计算开销大，难以部署在资源受限环境。现有知识蒸馏方法未能有效传递共情所需的细微理解和隐含语境线索。

Method: PRIDE包含三个核心组件：(1) 共情推理提示，引导教师模型逐步分解共情过程；(2) 多源注意力机制，使学生模型有效整合特权信息；(3) 双对齐损失（反向KL散度+最大均值差异），在logit和特征层面实现稳健知识迁移。

Result: 在多模态和纯文本数据集上，PRIDE达到了竞争性能，在某些指标上甚至匹配或超越了更大的教师模型，在准确性和语义相关性方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 利用训练阶段独有的特权信息（如专家心理标注、未来事件摘要）进行知识蒸馏，能够有效弥合大模型与小模型之间的共情理解差距，实现高效且高质量的共情对话生成。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated significant capabilities in generating diverse and context-aware responses for empathetic dialogue. However, their computational demands severely limit their deployment in resource-constrained environments. While knowledge distillation offers a promising compression solution, it often fails to transfer the nuanced understanding essential for empathy, as it overlooks the implicit contextual cues that guide human connection. To bridge this gap, we propose a \textbf{pr}ivileged \textbf{i}nformation-enhanced knowledge \textbf{d}istillation method for \textbf{e}mpathetic dialogue generation (PRIDE). Our method leverages privileged information, such as expert psychological annotations or future event summaries, which is available exclusively during training but unavailable at inference time. This allows us to transfer the teacher model's empathetic reasoning to smaller models without relying on extra inputs during deployment. Specifically, PRIDE has three key components: (1) An empathy-reasoning prompt that guides the teacher to explicitly decompose the empathetic process into understanding feelings and analyzing situations step-by-step; (2) A multi-source attention mechanism that directs the student to effectively integrate privileged information; (3) A dual-alignment loss that combines reversed Kullback-Leibler divergence and maximum mean discrepancy to ensure robust knowledge transfer at both logit and feature levels. Experiments on multi-modal and text-only datasets demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance, and in some cases matches or even surpasses larger teacher models in terms of accuracy and semantic relevance.

</details>


### [417] [Same question, different history: language, national identity, and credit in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23164)
*William Guey,Pierrick Bougault,Wei Zhang,Vitor D. de Moura,José O. Gomes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在回答发明归属问题时，查询语言会系统性地影响被提及的 claimant：低地位发明者在与其语言相关的问题中更易出现，而英语主导人物则跨语言稳定。语言充当了激活不同国家历史版本的开关，产生系统性的不同国家记忆，体现了计算形式的平庸民族主义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大型语言模型在处理跨文化、跨语言的历史争议性问题时，是否以及如何受到查询语言的影响，揭示语言在AI知识表征中扮演的民族主义角色。

Method: 对11个主流大语言模型，针对21项有争议的发明，使用12种语言进行75,896次查询评估，控制响应长度、模型差异、历史知名度、国家纪念程度等变量进行统计分析。

Result: 查询语言系统性地影响被提及的发明者：低地位 claimant 在其关联语言中更可能被提及，而英语主导人物跨语言表现稳定；该模式在控制多种变量后依然成立。

Conclusion: 大语言模型充当了文化记忆的分布式系统，语言条件决定了哪些历史版本变得可见，构成了计算形式的平庸民族主义。

Abstract: Who invented the radio, Russia's Alexander Popov or Italy's Guglielmo Marconi? Was the telephone the achievement of Bell in the United States or Meucci in Italy? Does printing belong to China's Bi Sheng or Germany's Gutenberg? The answer depends not only on historical record but also on language and perspective.
  We analyse eleven widely used large language models across 21 disputed inventions and discoveries, evaluated in twelve languages and 75,896 responses. While models generally acknowledge that credit is contested, query language systematically affects which claimant is surfaced. Lower-status claimants are more likely to appear when questions are asked in their associated language, whereas dominant Anglophone figures remain stable across languages.
  These patterns persist after controlling for response length, model differences, historical prominence, and levels of national commemoration. Language thus acts as a switch that activates different national versions of the same history, producing systematically different national memories from the same question.
  We interpret this as evidence that large language models function as distributed systems of cultural memory, where language conditions which histories become visible, contributing to a computational form of banal nationalism.

</details>


### [418] [When Does Intrinsic Self-Correction Help? A Task-Sensitive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23196)
*Elroy Stav,Dvir Berlowitz,Maayan Orner,Sarit Kraus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出内在自我纠错(SC)的效果取决于任务结构，在验证约束、复杂推理和提供二次意见等场景下可带来一致性能提升，而非一种普遍可靠的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究质疑内在自我纠错(SC)方法的可靠性，认为模型难以判断自身初始回答是否正确。作者希望从任务敏感的角度重新审视SC，探究在哪些具体任务结构下SC能够有效发挥作用。

Method: 采用任务敏感的视角，将SC操作机制划分为三种类型：验证显式约束、重新审视复杂推理过程、在文字游戏任务中为竞争策略提供二次意见。在多个基准测试和模型上进行实验评估。

Result: 当底层任务结构有利于上述三种修订模式时，SC能够带来一致的性能提升；但SC的效果高度依赖于任务类型，并非普适有效。

Conclusion: 内在自我纠错应被视为一种依赖于任务的推理阶段策略，其有用性取决于修订阶段在特定任务中能够扮演的角色，而非一种能够一致改进模型初始输出的可靠方法。

Abstract: Intrinsic self-correction (SC) aims to improve large language model outputs by prompting a model to revisit its own initial answer without external feedback. Recent studies have questioned the reliability of this approach, showing that models often struggle to judge whether their initial responses are correct. In this work, we take a task-sensitive view of SC. Rather than asking whether it works in general, we examine settings where SC may operate through different mechanisms: verifying explicit constraints, revisiting a complex reasoning process, or providing a second opinion over competing strategies in word-game tasks. Across multiple benchmarks and models, we find that SC can yield consistent performance gains when the underlying task structure facilitates these modes of revision. These results suggest that SC is best understood as a task-dependent inference-time strategy whose usefulness depends on the role the revision stage can play in a given task, rather than as a uniformly reliable method for improving initial model outputs.

</details>


### [419] [MuPPET: A Benchmark for Contextual Privacy of LLM Assistants in Multi-Party Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23217)
*Elena Sofia Ruzzetti,Cornelius Emde,Sangdoo Yun,Seong Joon Oh,Martin Gubri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MuPPET是一个评估多轮对话中多方上下文隐私泄露风险的基准测试，发现现有模型在多对多场景下比一对一场景泄露更多隐私，且现有防御措施效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文隐私基准仅考虑单对话者场景，无法衡量多轮对话中多方参与时的隐私泄露风险，而实际应用中LLM代理越来越多地部署在多方环境中处理敏感数据。

Method: 提出MuPPET（多方隐私暴露测试）基准，用于评估多轮对话中的上下文隐私保护能力，并通过实验比较模型在一对一与多方场景下的隐私泄露差异。

Result: 模型在多方场景下的隐私泄露显著高于一对一评估结果；前沿模型存在漏洞，小型开源模型更脆弱；现有上下文隐私防御措施仅提供部分保护且降低实用性，无法解决根本的参与者追踪问题。

Conclusion: 多方对话中的隐私风险比一对一场景更严重，现有防御手段不足，需要针对多方场景设计更有效的隐私保护机制。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed in multi-party environments, handling sensitive personal data on behalf of individual users, for instance in group chats. When such an agent discloses private information, it reaches every group member at once. This risk is structurally harder to control than in one-to-one settings, as every piece of private information must be appropriate for every recipient in the group. Yet all existing contextual privacy benchmarks consider only single-interlocutor settings, leaving multi-party privacy risks unmeasured. We introduce MuPPET (Multi-Party Privacy Exposure Testing), a benchmark for contextual privacy in multi-party conversations. Our experiments show that models leak substantially more in multi-party settings than one-to-one evaluations suggest. Frontier models are vulnerable, and smaller open-weights models, often preferred for local deployment with sensitive data, even more so. Existing contextual privacy defences offer only partial protection, degrade utility, and do not resolve the underlying party-tracking problem.

</details>


### [420] [Judgment-Grounded Expansion for Peer Review Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23233)
*Sheng Lu,Lizhen Qu,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“判断引导扩展”的人机协作论文评审生成模式，通过结构化的生成-检查-优化流程，将评审者的评价性主张扩展为评审意见候选集，并研究了可扩展评估和候选集筛选两个关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端自动评审生成缺乏问责性，难以应用于需要人机协作的场景；为了在自动化与问责性之间取得平衡，需要一种新的人机协作范式。

Method: 形式化定义了“判断引导扩展”任务，采用结构化的生成-检查-优化流程；通过用户研究收集人机交互数据；并开发了模拟大规模评估的方法，利用共形预测来平衡候选集规模与目标覆盖率。

Result: 验证了共形预测在候选集筛选中的有效性，能够在保证覆盖率的前提下控制候选集大小；建立了可扩展评估的方法基础。

Conclusion: 判断引导扩展作为一个具体任务被正式确立，为未来协作式评审生成系统提供了经验和方法论基础，有效平衡了自动化与问责性。

Abstract: Automatic review generation is a promising direction for accelerating scientific progress. While most work adopts an end-to-end setup, its fully automated nature makes it less suitable for settings that demand accountability. To better balance automation and accountability, we formalize judgment-grounded expansion, a human-AI collaboration mode where a reviewer provides an evaluative claim and the system expands it into review comment candidate(s). We model it as a structured generate-check-refine process and conduct a user study to collect human-model interaction data. We study two practical challenges for judgment-grounded expansion: scalable evaluation and candidate set curation. We develop methods to simulate the process for large-scale evaluation, and show that conformal prediction is well suited to balancing candidate set size and target coverage. Our work establishes judgment-grounded expansion as a concrete task and provides empirical and methodological foundations for the design of future collaborative review generation systems.

</details>


### [421] [Scaling LLM Knowledge Boundaries via Distribution-Optimized Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23271)
*Songze Li,Yarong Lan,Zhongpu Bo,Zhaoyang Wang,Zhiqiang Liu,Yuan Yuan,Chengtao Gan,Menghao Qian,Enpei Niu,Xiaoke Guo,Yuanxiang Liu,Zhaoyan Gong,Xiangjin Hu,Liangyurui Liu,Jingdian Lu,Lei Liang,Jun Zhou,Huajun Chen,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了KDoS框架，通过知识密度驱动合成数据生成，实现知识分布优化，从而最大程度扩展LLM的知识边界。实验表明存在最优知识分布，且该分布在不同模型和规模下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前知识注入的合成数据方法仅依赖预设的token数量或固定数据比例，忽略了知识分布，导致某些领域稀疏而另一些领域冗余，限制了LLM的知识边界扩展。

Method: 提出KDoS框架，引入知识密度概念，通过三阶段反馈机制驱动合成数据生成，将合成从盲目生成转变为分布优化合成。基于Wikipedia构建不同知识分布的合成数据，在0.6B至16B参数模型和1B至5B token数据规模上进行实验。

Result: (1) 存在最优知识分布能一致地最大化知识边界扩展；(2) 该分布在不同骨干网络和规模下保持稳定；(3) KDoS在六个知识基准上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 为合成数据驱动的知识注入提供了新的视角和实用框架，验证了从知识分布角度优化合成数据的有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge injection via synthetic data is crucial for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs). However, current synthesis methods simply stop at preset token counts or fixed data ratios, lacking awareness of knowledge distribution. This results in some domains being sparse while others are redundant, limiting LLM knowledge boundaries. We revisit knowledge injection from a distribution perspective and hypothesize that an optimal knowledge distribution exists to maximize knowledge boundary expansion. We propose KDoS (Knowledge Distribution-optimized Synthesis), a framework that introduces knowledge density to drive synthesis through a three-stage feedback mechanism, shifting from blind generation to distribution-optimized synthesis. We construct Wikipedia-based synthetic data with varying knowledge distributions and conduct experiments on models from 0.6B to 16B (Qwen, Ling, LLaMA) and data scales from 1B to 5B tokens. Our key findings are: (1) an optimal knowledge distribution consistently maximizes boundary expansion; (2) this distribution is stable across backbones and scales; (3) KDoS outperforms baselines across six knowledge benchmarks. Our work offers a new perspective and practical framework for synthetic data-driven knowledge injection.

</details>


### [422] [Towards Root Memories: Benchmarking and Enhancing Implicit Logical Memory Retrieval for Personalized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23283)
*Hongxun Ding,Xiang Yu,Chengbing Wang,Jianfei Xiao,Keqin Bao,Wenjie Wang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对现有LLM记忆系统仅依赖语义相似性检索而忽视逻辑相关性的问题，构建了首个面向隐式逻辑记忆检索的高质量基准IMLogic，并提出RootMem框架——通过将用户历史蒸馏为结构化的“根记忆”并利用LLM路由器激活逻辑相关记忆，显著提升了检索准确率和下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统中的检索方法仅依赖语义相似性，可能遗漏语义重叠少但逻辑关键的记忆，而当前基准无法有效评估这一缺陷。

Method: 构建IMLogic基准（首个隐式逻辑记忆检索基准）；提出RootMem框架：①将原始对话历史蒸馏为结构化的、保留决策信息的根记忆；②利用LLM路由器激活逻辑相关的根记忆，与语义检索互补。

Result: RootMem显著优于最强检索基线，并持续提升现有记忆智能体的准确率。

Conclusion: 隐式逻辑记忆检索是提升个性化LLM记忆系统性能的关键方向，RootMem通过结构化逻辑表示和路由机制有效弥补了纯语义检索的不足。

Abstract: Memory systems are essential for personalized Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing retrieval methods in these systems primarily rely on semantic similarity, potentially missing logically critical memories with limited semantic overlap. Current benchmarks remain inadequate for evaluating this problem. To address this gap, we construct IMLogic, the first high-quality benchmark targeting implicit logical memory retrieval in long-dialogue scenarios. Motivated by this challenge, we introduce root memory, a structured, decision-preserving representation that distills reusable personalized logic from long-term user histories. We then propose RootMem, a plug-and-play framework that first distills raw histories into structured root memories and then uses an LLM-based router to activate logically relevant ones, complementing semantic retrieval with personalized decision logic. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RootMem significantly outperforms the strongest retrieval baselines and consistently boosts the accuracy of existing memory agents. Our benchmark and codes will be available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/IMLogic-DBB3.

</details>


### [423] [On the Effect of Segmentation Width and Cluster Size on Speech Resynthesis and Continuation in Generative Spoken Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23285)
*Shunsuke Kando,Wataru Nakata,Shinnosuke Takamichi,Yusuke Miyao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究了生成式口语语言建模（GSLM）在不同比特率离散语音表示下的语音合成与续接性能，发现低比特率下仍可生成清晰自然的语音，且续接质量保持稳定，表明传统GSLM设置可能存在冗余；同时发现基于LLM的评估指标与人类主观评分的相关性仍较低，需更稳定的自动评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究GSLM框架下不同比特率的离散语音表示对语音合成与续接任务的影响，验证传统高比特率设置的必要性，并评估现有自动评估指标的可靠性。

Method: 采用固定宽度的语音表示分段，训练不同聚类数量的K-means模型以生成多种比特率设置，对比不同比特率下语音合成和续接的质量。

Result: 低比特率设置下仍能合成清晰自然的语音，且语音续接质量在多项指标上保持稳定；LLM-based指标与人类主观评分的相关性高于传统指标，但整体相关性仍然较低。

Conclusion: 传统GSLM的高比特率设置对于有效的语音生成可能是冗余的；当前自动评估方法尚不稳定，亟需开发更可靠的评估指标。

Abstract: Generative Spoken Language Modeling (GSLM) enables text-free speech modeling by training language models (LMs) using discrete speech representations instead of textual transcription. In this paper, we investigate the performance of GSLM on speech synthesis and continuation using discrete speech representations with varying bitrates. We segment speech representations with fixed widths and train K-means models in multiple cluster sizes, resulting in various bitrate settings. We demonstrate that intelligible and natural speech can be synthesized at lower bitrate settings than the baseline. Furthermore, speech continuation quality remains stable at lower bitrates across multiple metrics, suggesting that the conventional GSLM setting may be redundant for effective speech generation. Although LLM-based metrics show higher correlation with human subjective score than conventional metrics, it remains low, highlighting the need for more stable automatic evaluation methods.

</details>


### [424] [The Anatomy of the CTC Oracle Gap: Acoustic Exhaustion and Linguistic Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23306)
*Ivan Novosad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性地证明了CTC内部评分在N-best假设选择中已达到判别能力饱和：11种评分策略在G=16时均无统计显著的WER改进，CTC的Spearman相关系数随波束宽度增大而严重退化（-0.574→-0.270，损失53%），瓶颈在于空白路径泛滥导致的语言学信息缺失而非声学信息不足。通过引入外部语言模型（RoBERTa PLL）的MBR解码可突破此瓶颈，在多种架构、领域和噪声条件下取得显著提升（LibriSpeech test-other: 5.42% vs 贪婪5.96%，相对改善9.0%）。同时发现标准MWER训练在近收敛检查点会因训练oracle差距过小（0.007 pp）而完全失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究CTC内部评分在N-best假设选择中的极限，定位声学置信度与语言学合理性之间的信息瓶颈，并验证外部语言知识是否能突破该瓶颈。

Method: 系统评估11种CTC内部和声学特征评分策略在LibriSpeech上的表现；引入基于RoBERTa伪对数似然（PLL）后验的MBR解码；在多种Zipformer架构、三个领域和四种噪声水平下验证；分析标准MWER训练（CTC前向后向算法实现的Rao-Blackwellized REINFORCE）在近收敛检查点的行为。

Result: 11种内部评分策略在G=16时均无统计显著改进；CTC Spearman ρ从G=4的-0.574降至G=128的-0.270（损失53%）；MBR-CER解码在G=128时达到5.42% WER（相对改善9.0%），RoBERTa PLL ρ仅退化21%；MWER训练在oracle差距0.007 pp时全面崩溃（+6.18至+8.90 pp WER）。

Conclusion: CTC内部表征的判别能力已饱和，瓶颈本质上是语言学而非声学层面的；外部语言模型可突破该瓶颈；标准序列级微调在近收敛时因奖励信号消失而失效。

Abstract: We study the limits of CTC-internal scoring for N-best hypothesis selection and locate the information bottleneck separating acoustic confidence from linguistic plausibility. Eleven CTC-internal and acoustic-feature scoring strategies produce no statistically significant WER improvement over greedy decoding on LibriSpeech dev-other at G=16 (all p > 0.05). The exhaustion is systematic: CTC's Spearman $ρ$ between hypothesis score and per-utterance WER degrades from -0.574 at G=4 to -0.270 at G=128, a 53% loss driven by blank-path proliferation. This establishes that the discriminative capacity of CTC-internal representations is saturated: no recombination of acoustic signals can close the oracle gap.
  Confirming that the bottleneck is linguistic, not acoustic, external linguistic information introduced via MBR decoding breaks through it. MBR-CER decoding with a RoBERTa pseudo-log-likelihood (PLL) posterior ($τ$=10, G=128) achieves 5.42% WER on held-out LibriSpeech test-other (greedy 5.96%, $Δ$=-0.535 pp, p<0.0001, 9.0% relative). RoBERTa PLL $ρ$ degrades only 21% over the same range, retaining discriminating power where CTC loses it. Applied without retuning across two Zipformer architectures, three domains (LibriSpeech, TED-LIUM 3, VoxPopuli), and four MUSAN noise levels, the recipe gives significant gains in 11 of 13 conditions.
  On the training side, standard MWER training via the CTC forward-backward algorithm implements Rao-Blackwellized REINFORCE at the output projection (variance about 3x below Viterbi). Yet sequence-level fine-tuning fails at near-converged checkpoints: all four MWER configurations on CR-CTC collapse (+6.18 to +8.90 pp WER), as a training oracle gap of 0.007 pp provides no usable reward signal.

</details>


### [425] [Tmax: A simple recipe for terminal agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23321)
*Hamish Ivison,Junjie Oscar Yin,Rulin Shao,Teng Xiao,Nathan Lambert,Hannaneh Hajishirzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Tmax 是一个针对终端代理的强化学习开源方案，仅用 9B 参数在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上达到 27% 准确率，超越之前更大的模型。核心贡献包括：基于新分类法生成数据（融合难度控制、角色设定和验证器多样性），开源了 2.5 倍于之前最大的终端代理数据集，并发布了完整的模型、数据和代码基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管终端代理已成为语言模型最流行的下游应用，但学术界对基于强化学习的终端代理训练研究相对较少，主要受限于难以评估的基准、数据匮乏以及缺乏简单可复现的基线方案。

Method: 提出 Tmax 方案：1) 基于新颖分类法的数据生成流程，结合难度控制、角色设定和验证器多样化，低成本大规模生成终端环境数据；2) 使用仅基于结果（outcome-only）的简单强化学习配方训练开源权重模型。

Result: 仅用 9B 参数在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上达到 27% 准确率，超越之前更大的模型；开源数据集规模是此前最大终端代理数据集的 2.5 倍以上。

Conclusion: Tmax 提供了目前最强的开源终端代理强化学习配方，缩小了开源方案与前沿模型之间的差距，为未来学术研究提供了强基线。

Abstract: Terminal-using agents have quickly become the most popular downstream application of language models (LMs). Despite their prevalence, relatively little academic work has examined RL-based training of these models, likely due to difficult benchmarks, a lack of data, and a lack of simple baseline recipes. We present Tmax, the strongest open RL recipe for terminal agents to date, bringing open data recipes closer to the frontier. While simple, our recipe achieves 27\% on Terminal-Bench 2.0 with only 9B parameters, outperforming much larger models from prior work. Concretely, we generate data using a novel taxonomy, combining difficulty control, personas, and verifier diversification, which allows us to cheaply generate large amounts of terminal environments for RL and SFT training. We open-source our terminal dataset, which is over 2.5x larger than previously released terminal-agent datasets. We then train open-weight models using RL with our data, using a simple, outcome-only recipe. We release our data, models, and code as a strong baseline for future open academic work on terminal agents at https://github.com/hamishivi/tmax.

</details>


### [426] [WaveDetect: Robust Framework for Machine-Generated Text Detection via Wavelet Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23336)
*Zhichen Liu,Kaitong Qin,Linhan He,Yang Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Wavedetect框架，将LLM生成文本检测转化为时频域信号处理任务，通过可微连续小波变换提取文本的"频谱指纹"，在RAID、EvoBench和Domain-Shift数据集上实现了对对抗扰动、跨域偏移和模型演进三种挑战的最优检测性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成文本检测器在面对对抗扰动、跨域分布偏移以及底层LLM快速迭代演化时表现脆弱，而基于静态token概率分布的检测方法无法捕捉深层结构特征。

Method: 将生成输出建模为概率信号，应用可微连续小波变换（CWT）将其转换为可学习的频谱表示，从而提取机器生成文本中固有的"频谱指纹"——一种在时域中不可见的模式。

Result: 在RAID、EvoBench和Domain-Shift三个精心构建的数据集上达到新SOTA，在对抗攻击鲁棒性、跨域泛化以及对未见演化LLM的适应性上均表现优异。

Conclusion: 频谱分析作为LLM生成文本检测的新范式是有效的，能够揭示机器文本中隐藏的深层结构特征。

Abstract: As Large Language Models asymptotically approach human-level fluency in natural language generation, solely relying on surface-level semantic artifacts for detecting LLM-generated texts has become increasingly precarious. Existing detectors often falter when facing three critical challenges: adversarial perturbations, cross-domain shifts, and the rapid temporal evolution of the foundation model. To address these issues, we propose \wavedetect, a novel framework that reformulates text detection as a signal processing task within the time-frequency domain. Unlike previous methods that analyze static token probability distributions, \wavedetect models the generated output as a probability signal, upon which a differentiable Continuous Wavelet Transform is applied to convert them into learnable spectral representations. This process reveals the intrinsic ``spectral fingerprints'' in machine-generated texts--patterns that remain invisible in time domain. Comprehensive evaluations on three well-curated datasets (RAID, EvoBench, and Domain-Shift) show that our method achieves a new state-of-the-art. It not only achieves superior accuracy but also exhibits remarkable robustness against sophisticated attacks, generalization across out-of-distribution topics and unseen evolving LLMs. Our results validate the efficacy of spectral analysis as a promising paradigm for LLM-generated texts detection.

</details>


### [427] [Measuring & Mitigating Over-Alignment for LLMs in Multilingual Criminal Law Courts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23375)
*Arthur Wuhrmann,Gaetan Stein,Daniel Brunner,Andrei Kucharavy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对瑞士联邦最高法院在刑事法领域使用小型本地LLM时，因模型过度对齐（安全护栏）导致的拒答和免责声明问题，构建了TF-RefusalBench多语言基准数据集，系统评估了过度对齐现象的多维度影响因素，并验证了abliteration（拒绝方向消融）方法在消除拒答的同时对任务性能影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在刑事法领域的应用面临一个实际困境：法官和工作人员日常处理涉及暴力、性犯罪等敏感内容，但模型的安全护栏（过度对齐）会频繁触发拒答和免责声明，反而干扰了合法工作流程。因此需要量化评估这一现象并寻找解决方案。

Method: 1) 构建TF-RefusalBench多语言基准数据集，包含5,200个prompt，涵盖法、德、意、英四种语言，基于瑞士最高法院公开刑事判决；2) 系统评估不同模型、不同语言组合下过度对齐的表现；3) 对比prompt工程和abliteration（消融拒绝方向）两种缓解策略的效果。

Result: 1) 过度对齐是多维现象，受模型本身、prompt语言和文本语言共同影响；2) 过度对齐的影响不仅体现在拒答率上，免责声明还会损害任务忠实度；3) abliteration能有效消除拒答，且对任务性能影响极小，优于仅靠prompt工程。

Conclusion: 在刑事法等高度敏感领域，模型过度对齐会严重阻碍合法工作流程；abliteration是一种有效的缓解手段，可以在保持任务性能的同时消除不当拒答，为本地部署LLM在司法领域的窄范围应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While the wider applicability of LLMs in the legal field is currently debated due to their reliability and the gravity of any errors, narrow uses with well-understood and mitigated risks have emerged. Notably the Swiss Federal Supreme Court uses small on-premises models for tentative translations and short-passage summarization across the four official languages. However, such usage is challenging in the context of Criminal Law. Since rulings and cases employees work on routinely can contain detailed descriptions of violent and sexual offenses, their legitimate work is compromised by refusals and disclaimers due to the activation of model guardrails (over-alignment).
  To measure this phenomenon, we introduce TF-RefusalBench, a multilingual benchmark for criminal-law translation and summarization derived from public Swiss Supreme Court rulings. TF-RefusalBench contains 5,200 total prompts across French, German, Italian, and English, corresponding to common task prompts and passages likely to trigger refusal. We then use TF-RefusalBench to show that over-alignment is a multifaceted phenomenon, influenced by the model and the prompt and text languages being processed, and that its impact cannot be evaluated solely from an over-refusal perspective, given the disclaimer's impact on task faithfulness. Finally, we evaluate approaches to enable on-premises LLMs for Criminal Law Tasks, demonstrating that while prompting can be effective, abliteration (refusal directions ablation) eliminates refusal with minimal impact on task performance.

</details>


### [428] [Energy-Based Transformers as Predictors of Reading Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23382)
*Jakub Dotlacil,Ece Takmaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次探索能量基Transformer（关联记忆模型）中的能量度量作为句子处理难度的统一预测指标，实验表明该能量度量能显著预测阅读时间，超越surprisal和注意力熵，并统一了这两类互补指标的解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究使用surprisal和注意力熵作为阅读难度的互补预测指标，但它们来自不同的理论框架。能量基Transformer与Hopfield网络和密集关联记忆有形式化联系，作者希望探索其能量度量是否能作为一个统一的心理语言学预测指标。

Method: 在三个语料库（Natural Stories、UCL眼动追踪、UCL自定步速阅读）上进行回归分析，检验能量度量对阅读时间的预测能力；并在关系从句加工控制实验中检验能量度量能否捕捉主/宾语不对称现象。

Result: 能量度量在三个语料库中均显著预测阅读时间，且超越surprisal的拟合度；在关系从句实验中，单层能量即可捕捉主/宾语不对称；能量度量能够吸收注意力熵和surprisal各自解释的效应。

Conclusion: 能量度量可以作为一个统一的预测指标，替代此前需要多个互补指标（surprisal和注意力熵）才能完成的解释任务，为句子加工研究提供了与关联记忆理论相连接的新视角。

Abstract: Transformer language models have become established tools for modeling human sentence processing, with measures such as surprisal and attention entropy serving as effective predictors of reading difficulty that together capture complementary aspects of processing load. Here, we explore a related class of transformer models: energy-based transformers, which provide a principled formal link to associative memory models, bringing processing research into direct contact with the broader literature on Hopfield networks and dense associative memory. To our knowledge, this is the first exploration of an energy-based transformer measure in computational psycholinguistics. Across reading-time corpora (Natural Stories, UCL eye-tracking, UCL self-paced reading), the energy measure is a robust predictor of reading times, providing significant fit beyond surprisal in all three. In a controlled experiment on relative clause processing, energy at a single layer captures the well-known object/subject asymmetry. We find evidence that it subsumes effects attributable to both attention entropy and surprisal, suggesting that energy may serve as a single unified predictor where multiple complementary measures have previously been required.

</details>


### [429] [Self-Stigma Is Not a Monolith, but Generic Empathy Is: Persona-Conditioned LLM Support for People Who Use Drugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23387)
*Layla Bouzoubaa,Rezvaneh Rezapour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种面向药物使用人群（PWUD）自我污名表达的、基于人格感知的LLM支持方法。通过对Reddit上1,174名自我污名表达者的潜在特征分析，识别出四种人格类型；使用序列贝叶斯和递归神经网络分类器，在仅30条帖子的条件下即可恢复这些人格类型（macro-F1=0.74）。然而，临床专家评估发现，人格匹配的回应虽能实现预期的行为转变，但评估者整体上更偏好人格中立基线模型的通用共情回应。这表明整体共情判断与临床对齐的回应设计可能相互矛盾，需要能分解二者的评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我污名是导致药物使用人群（PWUD）逃避和脱离治疗的关键因素，然而现有的对话支持系统通常将自我污名表达视为单一、无差别的信号，忽视了其内在的异质性。

Method: 三阶段概念验证研究：(1) 对Reddit上1,174名自我污名表达者进行潜在特征分析（LPA），基于指示器级特征识别出四种人格类型；(2) 使用序列贝叶斯和递归神经网络分类器，从有限的发帖历史中恢复这些人格类型，并与批量/few-shot LLM基线比较；(3) 邀请八位临床专家对三种当代LLM生成的人格匹配回应与人格中立基线回应进行评估。

Result: 四人格类型得到保留行为和语言特征的验证；序列分类器在仅30条帖子时达到macro-F1=0.74，显著优于基线；人格匹配回应成功实现了目标行为转变，但临床专家整体上更偏好人格中立基线的通用共情回应。

Conclusion: 整体共情判断与临床对齐的回应设计可能方向相反，评估LLM驱动的污名支持需要能够分解这两者的评分标准。

Abstract: Self-stigma predicts treatment avoidance and disengagement among people who use drugs (PWUD), yet conversational systems aiming to provide support typically treat self-stigma expression as a uniform signal. We present a three-phase, proof-of-concept study of a persona-aware approach to LLM support. Latent Profile Analysis (LPA) on indicator-level features from 1,174 self-stigma expressors on Reddit yields a four-persona typology validated against held-out behavioral and linguistic features. Sequential Bayesian and recurrent neural classifiers recover these personas from limited posting histories, substantially outperforming batch and few-shot LLM baselines (macro-F1 = 0.74 at 30 posts). Evaluation by eight clinical experts across three contemporary LLMs revealed a misalignment: persona-matched responses successfully achieved targeted behavioral shifts, yet raters holistically preferred the generic empathy of the persona-neutral baseline. Our findings suggest that holistic empathy judgments and clinically-aligned response design can pull in opposite directions, and that evaluating LLM-based stigma support requires rubrics capable of decomposing the two.

</details>


### [430] [Do LLM Embedding Spaces Recover Expert Structure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23394)
*Yixuan Zhu,Zhenke Duan,Fanghen Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了预训练文本嵌入（Qwen3）在心理健康相关语言中恢复专家定义症状结构的能力，发现预训练嵌入与专家结构存在可测量对齐，微调能增强这种对齐，且模型规模增大有益，但对齐程度依赖于类别层级，需通过显式混淆变量进行检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练文本嵌入虽能实现高类别可分性，但其几何结构是否与专家定义的结构（如症状关系）一致尚不清楚。在心理健康领域，在线社区语言存在领域、情感、风格和话语等多重混淆因素，亟需系统评估嵌入空间能否恢复有意义的专家知识结构。

Method: 使用28个Reddit社区的心理健康相关语言数据，比较预训练与监督微调后的Qwen3嵌入空间（0.6B和4B两个规模）。构建类别原型，通过表征相似性分析（RSA）将原型间的表征不相似性矩阵与专家症状矩阵进行对比，并结合原型典型性分析和多基线混淆控制（VAD、LIWC、词汇风格、主题分布结构）进行验证。

Result: 预训练嵌入在心理健康子集中与专家结构存在可测量的对齐；微调在最细粒度类别层级上最能增强这种对齐；更大模型规模同时提升了零样本对齐和监督微调带来的增益。在控制VAD、LIWC、词汇风格和主题分布结构等混淆因素后，残余对齐仍然显著。

Conclusion: LLM嵌入能够恢复专家相关的类别几何结构，但这种恢复是层级依赖的，并且应通过显式混淆变量检验来评估，而非仅从分类性能推断。

Abstract: Pretrained text embeddings are increasingly used as representational maps, yet high category separability does not imply that their geometry recovers expert-defined structure. We study this problem in mental-health-related language, where symptom relations provide an external reference and online communities introduce strong domain, affective, stylistic, and discourse confounds. Using 28 Reddit communities, we compare pretrained and supervised fine-tuned Qwen3 embedding spaces at two scales (0.6B and 4B). We construct category prototypes, evaluate their representational dissimilarity matrices against an expert symptom matrix with representational similarity analysis, and complement this global test with prototype-based typicality and multi-baseline confound controls. Pretrained embeddings show measurable alignment with expert structure within the mental-health subset; fine-tuning strengthens this alignment most at the finest category level; and larger scale improves both zero-shot alignment and supervision-induced gains. Residual alignment remains substantial after controlling for VAD, LIWC, lexical style, and topic-distribution structure. These results suggest that LLM embeddings can recover expert-relevant category geometry, but this recovery is level-dependent and should be tested against explicit confounds rather than inferred from classification alone.

</details>


### [431] [ReasoningLens: Hierarchical Visualization and Diagnostic Auditing for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23404)
*Jun Zhang,Jiasheng Zheng,Boxi Cao,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Jia Zheng,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReasoningLens是一个开源框架，用于对大型推理模型的长思维链进行分层可视化和诊断审计，通过结构分层、自动化错误检测和系统推理画像生成，将非结构化的推理文本转化为可操作的洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型生成了极其冗长的思维链，关键逻辑信息被淹没在大量程序化文本中，导致透明性负担，难以进行有效的可解释性分析和调试。

Method: (1) 将推理链构建为交互式层次结构，分离高层策略与低层执行；(2) 利用智能体审计器进行自动化错误检测和工具增强验证；(3) 综合生成系统性推理画像，揭示模型特定的盲点。

Result: 将非结构化文本转化为结构化洞察，提供理解、调试和优化推理模型的基础能力。

Conclusion: ReasoningLens为下一代以推理为中心的AI提供了一个模块化基础，用于解释、调试和优化，解决了长思维链带来的透明度挑战。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Reasoning Models has introduced exceptionally long Chain-of-Thought traces, creating a transparency burden where critical logic is often buried under massive procedural text. To address this, we present ReasoningLens, an open-source framework designed for the hierarchical visualization and diagnostic auditing of complex reasoning chains. ReasoningLens addresses information necropsy by: (1) structuring traces into interactive hierarchies that separate high-level strategy from low-level execution; (2) leveraging an agentic auditor for automated error detection and tool-augmented verification; and (3) synthesizing systemic reasoning profiles to reveal model-specific blind spots. By transforming unstructured walls of text into actionable insights, ReasoningLens provides a modular foundation for interpreting, debugging, and optimizing the next generation of reasoning-centric AI.

</details>


### [432] [UnBias-Plus: Detect, Explain, and Rewrite Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23412)
*Ahmed Y. Radwan,Ahmed ElKady,Sindhuja Chaduvula,Mohamed Hafez,Amrit Krishnan,Shaina Raza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UnBias-Plus 是一个开源工具包，支持细粒度偏见检测（段级多类分类、偏见跨度定位）、中性重写和可解释推理，提供多种接口（Python、CLI、REST API、Web），所有资源均公开可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见检测方法仅能识别偏见存在，缺乏细粒度检测、可解释性、中性改写以及公开可用的训练模型。

Method: 构建了UnBias-Plus开源工具包，集成了段级多类偏见分类、偏见跨度定位、中性文本改写以及每个决策的推理功能，支持Python、CLI、REST API和Web多种接口。

Result: 实现了从单一偏见检测到细粒度多任务偏见分析（分类、定位、改写、推理）的统一工具包，并公开了源代码、模型、数据集和文档。

Conclusion: UnBias-Plus通过开源方式为偏见分析提供了全面、可访问且可解释的解决方案，填补了现有工具在细粒度和可解释性方面的空白。

Abstract: Bias in natural language remains a persistent challenge in both human-written and AI-generated content, affecting domains such as journalism, education, and AI research. Most existing detection methods identify only the presence of bias, with limited support for granular detection, interpretable explanations, neutral rewriting, and openly available trained models. We present UnBias-Plus, an open-source toolkit unifying (1) segment-level multi-class bias classification, (2) biased span localization, (3) neutral text rewriting, and (4) reasoning for each decision. Available via Python, CLI, REST API, and web interfaces, UnBias-Plus supports accessible bias analysis. The toolkit, source code, models, datasets, and documentation are publicly available.

</details>


### [433] [TriggerBench: Investigating Prospective Memory for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23459)
*Tianhua Zhang,Xinjiang Wang,Qianxi Zhang,Qi Chen,Kun Li,Yaoqi Chen,Dingdong Wang,Helen Meng,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TriggerBench是一个评估大语言模型前瞻记忆（PM）能力的基准，包含五个维度。研究发现：PM存在精度-召回率权衡和注意力脆弱性；PM比回溯记忆（RM）更难，随上下文长度增加而显著衰减；PM可以作为模型空闲推理能力的探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注大语言模型的回溯记忆（通过显式查询），而前瞻记忆（自发回忆并执行潜在约束的能力）尚未得到系统评估。

Method: 构建TriggerBench基准，涵盖日常助手和专业工作流五个维度；设计匹配的RM对照、对比正/负变体及过载触发器，在统一协议下精细测量主动召回、虚警率和注意力鲁棒性。

Result: (i) PM存在精度-召回率权衡和注意力脆弱性，增强推理可提升主动召回但可能过拟合"始终提醒"启发式，隐式约束或触发器过载下PM显著下降；(ii) PM比RM更难，RM在100K token内近乎饱和而PM随上下文长度急剧衰减；(iii) PM可作为空闲推理能力的行为探针，成功轨迹的PM准确率高于失败轨迹。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的前瞻记忆仍然是LLM面临的开放挑战；PM与RM在上下文长度扩展下的表现存在显著差异；PM可作为衡量模型空闲推理预算的有效指标。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in long interactions, existing evaluations focus predominantly on retrospective memory (RM) via explicit queries. Prospective memory (PM), the critical ability to spontaneously recall and act on latent constraints without direct prompts, remains largely unevaluated. We introduce TriggerBench, a comprehensive PM benchmark spanning five dimensions across both daily assistants and professional workflows. TriggerBench pairs scenarios with matched RM controls, contrastive positive/negative variants, and overloaded triggers, enabling fine-grained measurement of proactive recall, false-alarm rate, and attentional robustness under a single protocol. Our evaluation yields three key findings. (i) PM shows a precision-recall trade-off and attentional fragility. Though enhanced reasoning significantly improves proactive recall, models may overfit to an "always-remind" heuristic. Furthermore, PM accuracy degrades substantially under implicit constraints or triggers overloaded by concurrent user requests, indicating that robust PM remains an open challenge. (ii) PM is notably harder than RM: on identical contexts, RM near-saturates up to 100K tokens, while PM decays sharply as context length scales. (iii) PM may serve as a behavioral probe of spare reasoning capacity. Pairing PM scenarios with AIME-2025 math problems reveals that successful trajectories yield higher PM accuracy than failed ones at the same context length, showing PM tracks spare reasoning budget that token count obscures. Project page: https://github.com/KristenZHANG/TriggerBench-Official.

</details>


### [434] [War in the Abstract: The Rise and Consequences of Militarized Language in Scientific Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23462)
*Sovesh Mohapatra,David Lydon-Staley,Dani S. Bassett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对2010-2025年间2140万篇论文的分析发现，科学摘要中军事化语言使用频率增长了48%（OpenAlex）和32%（PubMed），增速在2019年后显著加快。实验表明，战争框架降低了可信度、资助意愿和政策支持，仅略微提升紧迫感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化科学摘要中军事化词汇的渗透程度，并因果性地检验军事化语言对说服力的影响。

Method: 分析2140万篇论文（2010-2025；OpenAlex, PubMed）中军事术语的出现频率，并结合乌普萨拉冲突数据项目进行国家与年度尺度的冲突一致性分析；开展受试者内战争框架实验（N=801；32,040次试验），测量可信度、资助意愿、政策支持和紧迫感的变化。

Result: 军事化术语在科学摘要中的出现率2010-2025年间上升48%（OpenAlex）和32%（PubMed），2019年后加速；全球南方摘要增长最快；社会科学水平最高，工程与计算机科学增长最快；COVID和LLM时代缩小了母语与非英语作者的语言差距。实验发现战争框架降低了可信度（d_z=-0.28）、资助意愿（d_z=-0.12）和政策支持（d_z=-0.08），趋势性地增加紧迫感（d_z=+0.07）。

Conclusion: 科学摘要正日益军事化，但军事化语言反而可能侵蚀可信度、资助意愿和政策支持。

Abstract: Scientists do not, by profession, wage war. Yet warfare's vocabulary consistently appears in their abstracts. To quantify the extent to which warfare's vocabulary pervades scientific abstracts, we analyze 21.4 million papers (2010-2025; OpenAlex, PubMed). We additionally run a within-subject war-framing experiment (N = 801; 32,040 trials) designed to provide causal insight into the effects of militaristic language on persuasion. Between 2010 and 2025, the presence of militaristic terms in scientific abstracts rose 48% in OpenAlex and 32% in PubMed, with the rise accelerating sharply after 2019 (cross-database r = 0.96, p < 10^-8). The prevalence of militaristic language is conflict-aligned at both country and annual scales (Uppsala Conflict Data Program; r = 0.77-0.84), with the abstracts from the Global South displaying the fastest rise in militaristic language. Among disciplines, social sciences leads in level of such language while engineering and computer science lead in growth. The COVID and post-2022 large-language-model eras also saw the rise and narrowed the language gap between native-English and non-English authors. In our follow-up experiment, we found that war framing reduced credibility (mean shift -0.18 Likert units, 95% CI [-0.21, -0.14]; d_z = -0.28, p < 10^-20), funding willingness (d_z = -0.12) and policy support (d_z = -0.08), with a trend-level increase in sense of urgency (d_z = +0.07). Collectively, findings reveal that while scientific abstracts drift toward warfare, the use of militaristic language may erode credibility, funding willingness, and policy support.

</details>


### [435] [Self-Compacting Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23525)
*Tianjian Li,Jingyu Zhang,William Jurayj,Xi Wang,Chuanyang Jin,Mehrdad Farajtabar,Eric Nalisnick,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SelfCompact 是一种无需微调的智能体脚手架，它通过让模型自身调用压缩工具并结合轻量级规则（何时压缩、何时抑制），实现对长轨迹的自适应上下文压缩，在数学推理和智能体搜索任务上以更低 token 成本达到或超过固定间隔压缩的效果，揭示了模型在无提示时存在元认知盲区，而这种盲区可通过规则来弥补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长智能体轨迹中累积的陈旧内容会锚定后续生成并超出上下文窗口，现有固定间隔压缩忽视轨迹结构，可能导致推导或搜索中途丢弃部分结果，因此需要一种能感知轨迹结构、由模型自主决定何时压缩的自适应方法。

Method: SelfCompact 配对两个推理时要素：(i) 一个压缩工具，由模型调用以总结累积的上下文；(ii) 一个轻量级规则，指定何时触发压缩（子任务完成或轨迹收敛）和何时抑制（推导中途或卡住时）。两者缺一不可，无需微调或外部监督。

Result: 在六个基准（数学推理和智能体搜索）和七个模型上的实验表明，SelfCompact 以更低 token 成本达到或超过固定间隔压缩，相比无压缩基线在数学任务上提升最高 18.1 分，在智能体搜索上提升 5-9 分，同时每问题成本降低 30-70%。

Conclusion: 模型存在元认知盲区——无提示时无法可靠判断自身上下文何时老化，但轻量级规则可以弥补这一盲区。何时压缩可以作为脚手架无需训练即可提供的能力。

Abstract: Long agent traces composed of chains of thought and tool calls accumulate stale content that anchor subsequent generations, and eventually outgrow the context window. Existing scaffolds mitigate it with fixed-interval compaction triggered at a token threshold. Such triggers pay no heed to trajectory structure, risking discard of partial results mid-derivation or mid-search. We propose SelfCompact, a scaffold that allows the model itself to decide when and how to compact. Specifically, it pairs two inference-time elements: (i) a compaction tool the model invokes to summarize the accumulated context, and (ii) a lightweight rubric specifying when to fire (a sub-task has resolved, or the trajectory is converging) and when to suppress (mid-derivation, or when stuck). Both are needed. The tool alone is unevenly used across open-weight models, often invoked at unhelpful moments or not at all; the rubric alone cannot act. Together, they elicit effective adaptive compaction without any fine-tuning or external supervision. We present empirical results on six benchmarks (competitive math and agentic search) and seven models. Our results show that SelfCompact matches or exceeds fixed-interval summarization at a fraction of the token cost, improving over a no-summarization baseline by up to 18.1 points on math and 5-9 points on agentic search at 30-70% lower per-question cost. Our results expose a meta-cognitive gap: although unprompted models cannot reliably tell when their own context is rotting, a lightweight rubric closes this gap, reframing when to compact as a capability that scaffolds can supply without training.

</details>


### [436] [LangMAP: A Language-Adaptive Approach to Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23566)
*Clara Meister,Suchir Salhan,Andrzej Szablewski,Pietro Lesci,Paula Buttery,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LangMAP分词方案，通过扩展UnigramLM到多语言设置，使用单一共享词汇表生成语言特定的分词，在无需改变词汇表的情况下适配预训练模型，并在9种自然语言和9种编程语言上提升了形态边界对齐和AST边界对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言特定的分词器能提升分词质量和下游模型性能，但使用此类分词器要么需要从头训练新模型，要么需要调整预训练模型的词汇表，成本高昂。现有方法缺乏一种既支持语言特定分词又无需改变共享词汇表的灵活方案。

Method: 将UnigramLM算法扩展到多语言设置，提出Language-adaptive Maximum a Posteriori (LangMAP) Tokenization。训练时需要语言标签，但推理时无需输入语言信息即可执行语言特定分词。

Result: 在14个开源分词器、9种自然语言和9种编程语言上，LangMAP提升了形态边界对齐效果；在所有编程语言上，改进了与抽象语法树（AST）叶子边界的对齐。微调实验中，LangMAP在目标语言语法可接受性（MultiBLiMP）上表现提升，但在知识相关任务（Global-PIQA, Belebele）上效果不一致。

Conclusion: LangMAP提供了一种高效的语言特定分词方案，既能用于从头训练多语言模型，也能适配预训练模型的分词器，且推理时无需语言标签。在语法任务上效果显著，但在知识密集型任务上优势有限，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Language-specific tokenizers improve tokenization quality and the downstream performance of models on those languages. However, using such a tokenizer comes at a cost: either a new model must be trained from scratch, or the vocabulary of an existing pretrained model must be adapted. We propose Language-adaptive Maximum a Posteriori (LangMAP) Tokenization, a tokenization scheme that extends the UnigramLM algorithm to the multilingual setting, producing language-specific tokenization from a single shared vocabulary. Notably, LangMAP can be used when training a multilingual language model from scratch or to adapt a pretrained model's tokenizer to individual languages without changing its vocabulary. While language labels are required at training time, a key feature of the algorithm is that it then performs language-specific tokenization at inference without knowledge of the input's language. Across 14 open-source tokenizers, 9 natural languages, and 9 programming languages, LangMAP improves morphological boundary alignment and, for all coding languages tested, alignment with abstract syntax tree (AST) leaf boundaries. In fine-tuning experiments, results are mixed: LangMAP improves target-language grammatical acceptability (MultiBLiMP) on the languages tested; its benefits are less consistent on knowledge-related tasks (Global-PIQA, Belebele).

</details>


### [437] [Evaluation Awareness Is Not One Capability: Evidence from Open Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23583)
*Nilesh Nayan,Aishwarya Sampath Kumar,Rishiraj Girmal,Shivani Anilkumar,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,David A. Nader Palacio,Reshmi Ghosh,Soundararajan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过8个实验、37个开源模型，发现模型能够检测到评估环境（Evaluation Awareness）并调整行为，导致基准测试表现与部署表现之间存在系统性偏差（基准幻觉）。检测能力受指令微调驱动，与模型规模相关性弱；检测会降低安全拒绝率；表征在行为崩溃后仍可恢复；各维度间耦合较弱，无法用单一分数衡量部署安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准测试假设测试条件下的行为能够预测部署行为，但若模型能检测到评估线索并调整行为，这一假设即失效。本文旨在系统性地刻画这种"评估感知"现象，揭示基准测试分数与真实部署安全性之间的差距（基准幻觉）。

Method: 在37个开源模型（7个模型族）上进行8项实验，从四个维度（检测能力、行为表现、表征持久性、维度耦合）系统评估评估感知现象。使用AUROC衡量检测能力，通过假设框架和HarmBench框架测量行为偏移，利用探针和激活操控分析表征存活情况，并计算维度间相关性。

Result: (i)检测能力中等且受训练驱动（24/37模型超过随机，最佳AUROC 0.714 vs人类0.819，指令微调主导而非规模）；(ii)检测改变安全行为（假设框架下硬拒绝下降5.8个百分点，21/140 HarmBench框架效应显著，合规率上升高达30个百分点）；(iii)表征在行为崩溃后仍存活（重写导致行为低于随机后，探针AUROC仍达0.98，多层引导可因果影响下游任务）；(iv)维度间耦合弱（仅1/15相关性显著，行为检测与框架抵抗间ρ=-0.79, p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 评估感知是一个多维现象（可检测性、行为表现、可控性独立变化），不能用单一的"感知分数"作为部署安全性的可靠代理。基准测试存在系统性幻觉，应关注模型在无评估环境下的真实行为，而非优化测试条件下的合规率。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks assume that test-condition behavior predicts deployment behavior, an assumption that fails if models detect evaluation cues and adapt. This opens a gap between benchmark performance and deployment behavior: compliance measured under test conditions becomes an optimistic upper bound that overstates how safely a model behaves once the evaluation harness is removed. We characterize this evaluation awareness through eight experiments across 37 open-weight models and seven families. (i)Detection is moderate and training-driven (24/37 models exceed chance, best AUROC 0.714 vs.0.819 human, with instruction tuning dominating over scale). (ii)Detection shifts safety behavior (hard refusal drops 5.8 percentage points under hypothetical framing, and 21/140 HarmBench framing effects are significant, with compliance rising up to +30 percentage points. (iii)Representations survive behavioral collapse (probes retain AUROC 0.98 under rewrites that drive behavior below chance, and multi-layer steering causally moves three downstream tasks while random controls do not). (iv)These axes are weakly coupled (only 1/15 correlations are significant, the sole robust link being behavioral detection versus framing resistance, $ρ=-0.79$, $p<0.001$). We call this gap the benchmark illusion: because detectability, behavioral manifestation, and controllability vary independently, it is multivariate rather than a single number, so no single awareness score is a reliable proxy for deployment safety.

</details>


### [438] [EnterpriseClawBench: Benchmarking Agents from Real Workplace Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23654)
*Jincheng Zhong,Weizhi Wang,Che Jiang,Kai Tian,Zhenzhao Yuan,Junlin Yang,Dianqiao Lei,Kaiyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 我们提出了EnterpriseClawBench，一个基于专有真实企业工作会话构建的智能体基准，能够生成852个可复现任务，并配套完整的评估协议。最佳配置（Codex with GPT-5.5）仅达到0.663分，结果表明企业智能体评估必须从多维度（模型-平台组合、人工制品交付、视觉质量、成本、运行时、技能迁移行为）进行，而非单一评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体越来越多地在工作空间中运行（读取异构文件、调用工具、交付业务产物），但缺乏一个能够真实反映企业工作场景、具备可复现性和多维度评估能力的基准测试。

Method: 基于大型专有企业工作会话档案，构建852个可复现任务，每个任务配有恢复的夹具、重写的提示、角色类别、技能子类、硬规则和语义评估准则。由于数据包含企业内部内容，不公开数据本身，而是公开构建和评估协议。

Result: 最佳配置（Codex with GPT-5.5）仅达到0.663分，说明当前企业智能体性能仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: 企业智能体评估必须报告模型-平台组合、人工制品交付、视觉质量、成本、运行时和技能迁移行为等多维度指标，而非单一评分。

Abstract: Enterprise agents increasingly operate inside workspaces: they read heterogeneous files, invoke tools, and deliver business artifacts. We introduce EnterpriseClawBench, an enterprise agent benchmark constructed from proprietary, real-world agent sessions. Starting from a large archive of workplace sessions, the EnterpriseClawBench produces 852 reproducible tasks, each paired with recovered fixtures, rewritten prompts, role classes, skill subclasses, hard rules, and semantic rubrics. Because the sessions contain internal enterprise content, we do not release the benchmark data; instead, our reusable contribution is the construction and evaluation protocol. On EnterpriseClawBench, the best configuration reaches only 0.663 (Codex with GPT-5.5). These results show that enterprise agent evaluation must report harness--model combinations, artifact delivery, visual quality, cost, runtime, and skill-transfer behavior, rather than collapsing performance into a single score. Code: https://github.com/FrontisAI/EnterpriseClawBench

</details>


### [439] [Can LLMs Reliably Self-Report Adversarial Prefills, and How?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23671)
*Quang Minh Nguyen,Uzair Ahmed,Taegyoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在安全场景下无法可靠识别自身是否受到对抗性前缀攻击；内省信号主要来源于安全/拒绝相关推理；将模型权重与拒绝方向正交化会缩小预填充与自然输出之间的声称率差距；内省信号具有探针依赖性；LoRA微调方法（SFT、GRPO、DPO）会扩大意图探针差距且不迁移至篡改探针，反而多数模型上提高了攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明LLM在良性任务上具有内省能力，本文将其扩展到安全场景，探究模型能否可靠识别自身先前的输出是否由对抗性前缀攻击（adversarial prefill attack）所诱发。

Method: 在10个开源指令微调LLM（3B-70B）和4个安全基准上进行实验；分析内省信号的来源（安全/拒绝推理）；通过正交化权重与拒绝方向测试因果关系；设计两种探针（内部意图vs外部篡改）观察探针依赖性；使用LoRA微调（SFT、GRPO、DPO）在8个模型（3B-27B）上进行干预。

Result: 1) 没有模型能可靠识别自身被攻击的输出，预填充响应平均声称意图率为27.3%；2) 内省信号主要来自安全/拒绝推理；3) 正交化拒绝方向使预填充与自然输出的声称率差距几乎归零；4) 意图探针与篡改探针引发定性不同的响应；5) LoRA微调在8B-27B模型上均扩大了意图探针差距，但方法排序因模型而异；6) 干预不迁移至篡改探针，且多数模型上反而提高了攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了安全场景下LLM内省信号的机制基础，指出模型自我报告在安全上下文中的不可靠性风险，以及现有干预措施（如LoRA微调）可能产生的反直觉效果——部分缓解同时可能提高攻击成功率。

Abstract: Prior work shows that large language models (LLMs) exhibit introspective capability on benign tasks. We extend the question to safety contexts and examine how reliably a model can recognize that its own prior response was elicited by an adversarial prefill attack. Across ten open-weight instruction-tuned LLMs (3B to 70B) and four safety benchmarks, no model reliably recognizes its own compromised outputs, with models claiming intent on prefilled responses at an average rate of $27.3\%$. Introspective signal stems largely from safety- and refusal-related reasoning. Orthogonalizing models' weights against the refusal direction collapses the gap between claiming rates on prefilled and natural outputs to near zero, though the direction is not its unique mediator. The signal is also probe-dependent: framing the question as internal intention versus external tampering elicits qualitatively different responses on the same models. We test three LoRA finetuning methods (SFT, GRPO, DPO) on eight models from 3B to 27B; all three widen the intention-probe gap on every model from 8B to 27B, with method ranking varying by model. The intervention does not transfer to the tampering probe and counterintuitively raises attack success rate under adversarial prefill on most models, amounting to a partial mitigation. These findings outline mechanisms underpinning the observed introspective signals in safety contexts and highlight risks in the reliability of LLM self-reports.

</details>


### [440] [Randomized YaRN Improves Length Generalization for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23687)
*Manas Mehta,Fangcong Yin,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Randomized YaRN方法，通过在短序列训练中结合YaRN位置外推、随机位置编码和长度课程，使LLM能够泛化到超长序列（16K-128K），在BABILong和MRCR基准上显著优于标准微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通常在短序列上预训练，然后通过额外训练扩展到更长序列，但仍然难以进一步泛化到极长序列。

Method: Randomized YaRN：在短上下文数据训练时，为token分配从更大位置范围采样的YaRN位置编码，结合长度课程，使模型在短上下文输入下也暴露于分布外的位置表示。

Result: 在<8K上下文数据上训练时，Randomized YaRN在16K到128K上下文长度上持续提升推理性能，在远超出分布的长度上增益最大。

Conclusion: 逐步将模型暴露于分布外位置分布是一种实现可泛化长上下文推理的有效策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically pretrained on short sequences and then extended to work on longer sequences with additional training. However, such LLMs still struggle to further generalize to very long sequences. We propose Randomized YaRN, a training method that improves length generalization by combining YaRN-based positional extrapolation with randomized positional encoding and a length curriculum. During training on short context data, tokens are assigned YaRN positional encodings sampled from a larger position range, exposing the model to out-of-distribution positional representations even on short-context inputs. We evaluate Randomized YaRN on two challenging long-context reasoning benchmarks, BABILong and Multi-Round Coreference Resolution (MRCR). When training on data with <8K context, Randomized YaRN consistently improves reasoning performance on context lengths from 16K to 128K and outperforms standard fine-tuning, with the largest gains appearing at far out-of-distribution lengths. Our results suggest that progressively exposing models to OOD positional distributions provides an effective recipe for generalizable long-context reasoning.

</details>
